==== src/Surface.zig ====
1	//! Surface represents a single terminal "surface". A terminal surface is
2	//! a minimal "widget" where the terminal is drawn and responds to events
3	//! such as keyboard and mouse. Each surface also creates and owns its pty
4	//! session.
5	//!
6	//! The word "surface" is used because it is left to the higher level
7	//! application runtime to determine if the surface is a window, a tab,
8	//! a split, a preview pane in a larger window, etc. This struct doesn't care:
9	//! it just draws and responds to events. The events come from the application
10	//! runtime so the runtime can determine when and how those are delivered
11	//! (i.e. with focus, without focus, and so on).
12	const Surface = @This();
74	/// The app that this surface is attached to.
75	app: *App,
76	
77	/// The windowing system surface and app.
78	rt_app: *apprt.runtime.App,
79	rt_surface: *apprt.runtime.Surface,
81	/// The font structures
82	font_grid_key: font.SharedGridSet.Key,
83	font_size: font.face.DesiredSize,
84	font_metrics: font.Metrics,
85	
86	/// This keeps track of if the font size was ever modified. If it wasn't,
87	/// then config reloading will change the font. If it was manually adjusted,
88	/// we don't change it on config reload since we assume the user wants
89	/// a specific size.
90	font_size_adjusted: bool,
91	
92	/// The renderer for this surface.
93	renderer: Renderer,
94	
95	/// The render state
96	renderer_state: rendererpkg.State,
97	
98	/// The renderer thread manager
99	renderer_thread: rendererpkg.Thread,
100	
101	/// The actual thread
102	renderer_thr: std.Thread,
103	
104	/// Mouse state.
105	mouse: Mouse,
106	
107	/// Keyboard input state.
108	keyboard: Keyboard,
133	/// The terminal IO handler.
134	io: termio.Termio,
135	io_thread: termio.Thread,
136	io_thr: std.Thread,
137	
138	/// Terminal inspector
139	inspector: ?*inspectorpkg.Inspector = null,
141	/// All our sizing information.
142	size: rendererpkg.Size,
144	/// The configuration derived from the main config. We "derive" it so that
145	/// we don't have a shared pointer hanging around that we need to worry about
146	/// the lifetime of. This makes updating config at runtime easier.
147	config: DerivedConfig,
155	/// This is set to true if our IO thread notifies us our child exited.
156	/// This is used to determine if we need to confirm, hold open, etc.
157	child_exited: bool = false,
158	
159	/// We maintain our focus state and assume we're focused by default.
160	/// If we're not initially focused then apprts can call focusCallback
161	/// to let us know.
162	focused: bool = true,
163	
164	/// Whether this surface may be visible. Unknown visibility is considered
165	/// visible so reporting remains conservative.
166	visible: bool = true,
171	/// True if the surface is in read-only mode. When read-only, no input
172	/// is sent to the PTY but terminal-level operations like selections,
173	/// (native) scrolling, and copy keybinds still work. Warn before quit is
174	/// always enabled in this state.
175	readonly: bool = false,
184	/// Search state
185	search: ?Search = null,
190	/// The effect of an input event. This can be used by callers to take
191	/// the appropriate action after an input event. For example, key
192	/// input can be forwarded to the OS for further processing if it
193	/// wasn't handled in any way by Ghostty.
194	pub const InputEffect = enum {
195	    /// The input was not handled in any way by Ghostty and should be
196	    /// forwarded to other subsystems (i.e. the OS) for further
197	    /// processing.
198	    ignored,
199	
200	    /// The input was handled and consumed by Ghostty.
201	    consumed,
202	
203	    /// The input resulted in a close event for this surface so
204	    /// the surface, runtime surface, etc. pointers may all be
205	    /// unsafe to use so exit immediately.
206	    closed,
207	};
209	/// The search state for the surface.
210	const Search = struct {
211	    state: terminal.search.Thread,
212	    thread: std.Thread,
213	
214	    pub fn deinit(self: *Search) void {
215	        // Notify the thread to stop
216	        self.state.stop.notify() catch |err| log.err(
217	            "error notifying search thread to stop, may stall err={}",
218	            .{err},
219	        );
220	
221	        // Wait for the OS thread to quit
222	        self.thread.join();
223	
224	        // Now it is safe to deinit the state
225	        self.state.deinit();
226	    }
227	};
229	/// Mouse state for the surface.
230	const Mouse = struct {
231	    /// The last tracked mouse button state by button, indexed by the
232	    /// button's enum value. `max` is the largest value, not a count, so
233	    /// the array needs one more slot than that to hold every button.
234	    click_state: [input.MouseButton.max + 1]input.MouseButtonState = @splat(.release),
235	
236	    /// The last mods state when the last mouse button (whatever it was) was
237	    /// pressed or release.
238	    mods: input.Mods = .{},
239	
240	    /// Gesture state for text selection.
241	    selection_gesture: terminal.SelectionGesture = .init,
242	
243	    /// The last x/y sent for mouse reports.
244	    event_point: ?terminal.point.Coordinate = null,
245	
246	    /// The pressure stage for the mouse. This should always be none if
247	    /// the mouse is not pressed.
248	    pressure_stage: input.MousePressureStage = .none,
249	
250	    /// Pending scroll amounts for high-precision scrolls
251	    pending_scroll_x: f64 = 0,
252	    pending_scroll_y: f64 = 0,
253	
254	    /// True if the mouse is hidden
255	    hidden: bool = false,
256	
257	    /// True if the mouse position is currently over a link.
258	    over_link: bool = false,
259	
260	    /// The last x/y in the cursor position for links. We use this to
261	    /// only process link hover events when the mouse actually moves cells.
262	    link_point: ?terminal.point.Coordinate = null,
263	
264	    /// Return the left-click pin only if it still belongs to the active screen.
265	    fn activeLeftClickPin(self: *const Mouse, screens: *const terminal.ScreenSet) ?*terminal.Pin {
266	        return self.selection_gesture.validatedLeftClickPin(screens);
267	    }
268	};
269	
270	/// Keyboard state for the surface.
271	pub const Keyboard = struct {
272	    /// The currently active key sequence for the surface. If this is null
273	    /// then we're not currently in a key sequence.
274	    sequence_set: ?*const input.Binding.Set = null,
275	
276	    /// The queued keys when we're in the middle of a sequenced binding.
277	    /// These are flushed when the sequence is completed and unconsumed or
278	    /// invalid.
279	    ///
280	    /// This is naturally bounded due to the configuration maximum
281	    /// length of a sequence.
282	    sequence_queued: std.ArrayList(termio.Message.WriteReq) = .empty,
283	
284	    /// The stack of tables that is currently active. The first value
285	    /// in this is the first activated table (NOT the default keybinding set).
286	    ///
287	    /// This is bounded by `max_active_key_tables`.
288	    table_stack: std.ArrayList(struct {
289	        set: *const input.Binding.Set,
290	        once: bool,
291	    }) = .empty,
292	
293	    /// The last handled binding. This is used to prevent encoding release
294	    /// events for handled bindings. We only need to keep track of one because
295	    /// at least at the time of writing this, its impossible for two keys of
296	    /// a combination to be handled by different bindings before the release
297	    /// of the prior (namely since you can't bind modifier-only).
298	    last_trigger: ?u64 = null,
299	};
301	/// The configuration that a surface has, this is copied from the main
302	/// Config struct usually to prevent sharing a single value.
303	const DerivedConfig = struct {
304	    arena: ArenaAllocator,
305	
306	    /// For docs for these, see the associated config they are derived from.
307	    original_font_size: f32,
308	    keybind: configpkg.Keybinds,
309	    abnormal_command_exit_runtime_ms: u32,
310	    clipboard_read: configpkg.ClipboardAccess,
311	    clipboard_write: configpkg.ClipboardAccess,
312	    clipboard_trim_trailing_spaces: bool,
313	    clipboard_paste_protection: bool,
314	    clipboard_paste_bracketed_safe: bool,
315	    clipboard_codepoint_map: configpkg.Config.RepeatableClipboardCodepointMap,
316	    copy_on_select: configpkg.CopyOnSelect,
317	    right_click_action: configpkg.RightClickAction,
318	    middle_click_action: configpkg.MiddleClickAction,
319	    confirm_close_surface: configpkg.ConfirmCloseSurface,
320	    cursor_click_to_move: bool,
321	    desktop_notifications: bool,
322	    font: font.SharedGridSet.DerivedConfig,
323	    mouse_interval: u64,
324	    mouse_hide_while_typing: bool,
325	    mouse_reporting: bool,
326	    mouse_scroll_multiplier: configpkg.MouseScrollMultiplier,
327	    mouse_shift_capture: configpkg.MouseShiftCapture,
328	    fullscreen: configpkg.Fullscreen,
329	    macos_non_native_fullscreen: configpkg.NonNativeFullscreen,
330	    macos_option_as_alt: ?input.OptionAsAlt,
331	    selection_clear_on_copy: bool,
332	    selection_clear_on_typing: bool,
333	    selection_word_chars: []const u21,
334	    vt_kam_allowed: bool,
335	    wait_after_command: bool,
336	    window_padding_top: u32,
337	    window_padding_bottom: u32,
338	    window_padding_left: u32,
339	    window_padding_right: u32,
340	    window_padding_balance: configpkg.Config.WindowPaddingBalance,
341	    window_height: u32,
342	    window_width: u32,
343	    title: ?[:0]const u8,
344	    title_report: bool,
345	    vt_window_resize_allowed: bool,
346	    links: []DerivedConfig.Link,
347	    link_osc8: bool,
348	    link_previews: configpkg.LinkPreviews,
349	    scroll_to_bottom: configpkg.Config.ScrollToBottom,
350	    notify_on_command_finish: configpkg.Config.NotifyOnCommandFinish,
351	    notify_on_command_finish_action: configpkg.Config.NotifyOnCommandFinishAction,
352	    notify_on_command_finish_after: Duration,
353	    key_remaps: input.KeyRemapSet,
354	
355	    const Link = struct {
356	        regex: oni.Regex,
357	        action: input.Link.Action,
358	        highlight: input.Link.Highlight,
359	    };
360	
361	    pub fn init(alloc_gpa: Allocator, config: *const configpkg.Config) !DerivedConfig {
362	        var arena = ArenaAllocator.init(alloc_gpa);
363	        errdefer arena.deinit();
364	        const alloc = arena.allocator();
365	
366	        // Build all of our links
367	        const links = links: {
368	            var links: std.ArrayList(DerivedConfig.Link) = .empty;
369	            defer links.deinit(alloc);
370	            for (config.link.links.items) |link| {
371	                var regex = try link.oniRegex();
372	                errdefer regex.deinit();
373	                try links.append(alloc, .{
374	                    .regex = regex,
375	                    .action = link.action,
376	                    .highlight = link.highlight,
377	                });
378	            }
379	
380	            break :links try links.toOwnedSlice(alloc);
381	        };
382	        errdefer {
383	            for (links) |*link| link.regex.deinit();
384	            alloc.free(links);
385	        }
386	
387	        return .{
388	            .original_font_size = config.@"font-size",
389	            .keybind = try config.keybind.clone(alloc),
390	            .abnormal_command_exit_runtime_ms = config.@"abnormal-command-exit-runtime",
391	            .clipboard_read = config.@"clipboard-read",
392	            .clipboard_write = config.@"clipboard-write",
393	            .clipboard_trim_trailing_spaces = config.@"clipboard-trim-trailing-spaces",
394	            .clipboard_paste_protection = config.@"clipboard-paste-protection",
395	            .clipboard_paste_bracketed_safe = config.@"clipboard-paste-bracketed-safe",
396	            .clipboard_codepoint_map = try config.@"clipboard-codepoint-map".clone(alloc),
397	            .copy_on_select = config.@"copy-on-select",
398	            .right_click_action = config.@"right-click-action",
399	            .middle_click_action = config.@"middle-click-action",
400	            .confirm_close_surface = config.@"confirm-close-surface",
401	            .cursor_click_to_move = config.@"cursor-click-to-move",
402	            .desktop_notifications = config.@"desktop-notifications",
403	            .font = try font.SharedGridSet.DerivedConfig.init(alloc, config),
404	            .mouse_interval = config.@"click-repeat-interval" * 1_000_000, // 500ms
405	            .mouse_hide_while_typing = config.@"mouse-hide-while-typing",
406	            .mouse_reporting = config.@"mouse-reporting",
407	            .mouse_scroll_multiplier = config.@"mouse-scroll-multiplier",
408	            .mouse_shift_capture = config.@"mouse-shift-capture",
409	            .fullscreen = config.fullscreen,
410	            .macos_non_native_fullscreen = config.@"macos-non-native-fullscreen",
411	            .macos_option_as_alt = config.@"macos-option-as-alt",
412	            .selection_clear_on_copy = config.@"selection-clear-on-copy",
413	            .selection_clear_on_typing = config.@"selection-clear-on-typing",
414	            .selection_word_chars = try alloc.dupe(u21, config.@"selection-word-chars".codepoints),
415	            .vt_kam_allowed = config.@"vt-kam-allowed",
416	            .wait_after_command = config.@"wait-after-command",
417	            .window_padding_top = config.@"window-padding-y".top_left,
418	            .window_padding_bottom = config.@"window-padding-y".bottom_right,
419	            .window_padding_left = config.@"window-padding-x".top_left,
420	            .window_padding_right = config.@"window-padding-x".bottom_right,
421	            .window_padding_balance = config.@"window-padding-balance",
422	            .window_height = config.@"window-height",
423	            .window_width = config.@"window-width",
424	            .title = config.title,
425	            .title_report = config.@"title-report",
426	            .vt_window_resize_allowed = config.@"vt-window-resize-allowed",
427	            .links = links,
428	            .link_osc8 = config.@"link-osc8",
429	            .link_previews = config.@"link-previews",
430	            .scroll_to_bottom = config.@"scroll-to-bottom",
431	            .notify_on_command_finish = config.@"notify-on-command-finish",
432	            .notify_on_command_finish_action = config.@"notify-on-command-finish-action",
433	            .notify_on_command_finish_after = config.@"notify-on-command-finish-after",
434	            .key_remaps = try config.@"key-remap".clone(alloc),
435	
436	            // Assignments happen sequentially so we have to do this last
437	            // so that the memory is captured from allocs above.
438	            .arena = arena,
439	        };
440	    }
441	
442	    pub fn deinit(self: *DerivedConfig) void {
443	        for (self.links) |*link| link.regex.deinit();
444	        self.arena.deinit();
445	    }
446	
447	    fn scaledPadding(self: *const DerivedConfig, x_dpi: f32, y_dpi: f32) rendererpkg.Padding {
448	        const padding_top: u32 = padding_top: {
449	            const padding_top: f32 = @floatFromInt(self.window_padding_top);
450	            break :padding_top @intFromFloat(@floor(padding_top * y_dpi / 72));
451	        };
452	        const padding_bottom: u32 = padding_bottom: {
453	            const padding_bottom: f32 = @floatFromInt(self.window_padding_bottom);
454	            break :padding_bottom @intFromFloat(@floor(padding_bottom * y_dpi / 72));
455	        };
456	        const padding_left: u32 = padding_left: {
457	            const padding_left: f32 = @floatFromInt(self.window_padding_left);
458	            break :padding_left @intFromFloat(@floor(padding_left * x_dpi / 72));
459	        };
460	        const padding_right: u32 = padding_right: {
461	            const padding_right: f32 = @floatFromInt(self.window_padding_right);
462	            break :padding_right @intFromFloat(@floor(padding_right * x_dpi / 72));
463	        };
464	
465	        return .{
466	            .top = padding_top,
467	            .bottom = padding_bottom,
468	            .left = padding_left,
469	            .right = padding_right,
470	        };
471	    }
472	};
474	/// Create a new surface. This must be called from the main thread. The
475	/// pointer to the memory for the surface must be provided and must be
476	/// stable due to interfacing with various callbacks.
477	pub fn init(
478	    self: *Surface,
479	    alloc: Allocator,
480	    config_original: *const configpkg.Config,
481	    app: *App,
482	    rt_app: *apprt.runtime.App,
483	    rt_surface: *apprt.runtime.Surface,
484	) !void {
485	    // Apply our conditional state. If we fail to apply the conditional state
486	    // then we log and attempt to move forward with the old config.
487	    var config_: ?configpkg.Config = config_original.changeConditionalState(
488	        app.config_conditional_state,
489	    ) catch |err| err: {
490	        log.warn("failed to apply conditional state to config err={}", .{err});
491	        break :err null;
492	    };
493	    defer if (config_) |*c| c.deinit();
494	
495	    // We want a config pointer for everything so we get that either
496	    // based on our conditional state or the original config.
497	    const config: *const configpkg.Config = if (config_) |*c| config: {
498	        // We want to preserve our original working directory. We
499	        // don't need to dupe memory here because termio will derive
500	        // it. We preserve this so directory inheritance works.
501	        c.@"working-directory" = config_original.@"working-directory";
502	        break :config c;
503	    } else config_original;
504	
505	    // Get our configuration
506	    var derived_config = try DerivedConfig.init(alloc, config);
507	    errdefer derived_config.deinit();
508	
509	    // Determine our DPI configurations so we can properly configure
510	    // font points to pixels and handle other high-DPI scaling factors.
511	    const content_scale = try rt_surface.getContentScale();
512	    const x_dpi = content_scale.x * font.face.default_dpi;
513	    const y_dpi = content_scale.y * font.face.default_dpi;
514	    log.debug("xscale={} yscale={} xdpi={} ydpi={}", .{
515	        content_scale.x,
516	        content_scale.y,
517	        x_dpi,
518	        y_dpi,
519	    });
520	
521	    // The font size we desire along with the DPI determined for the surface
522	    const font_size: font.face.DesiredSize = .{
523	        .points = config.@"font-size",
524	        .xdpi = @intFromFloat(@round(x_dpi)),
525	        .ydpi = @intFromFloat(@round(y_dpi)),
526	    };
527	
528	    // Setup our font group. This will reuse an existing font group if
529	    // it was already loaded.
530	    const font_grid_key, const font_grid = try app.font_grid_set.ref(
531	        &derived_config.font,
532	        font_size,
533	    );
534	    errdefer app.font_grid_set.deref(font_grid_key);
535	
536	    // Build our size struct which has all the sizes we need.
537	    const size: rendererpkg.Size = size: {
538	        var size: rendererpkg.Size = .{
539	            .screen = screen: {
540	                const surface_size = try rt_surface.getSize();
541	                break :screen .{
542	                    .width = surface_size.width,
543	                    .height = surface_size.height,
544	                };
545	            },
546	
547	            .cell = font_grid.cellSize(),
548	            .padding = .{},
549	        };
550	
551	        const explicit: rendererpkg.Padding = derived_config.scaledPadding(
552	            x_dpi,
553	            y_dpi,
554	        );
555	        if (derived_config.window_padding_balance != .false) {
556	            size.balancePadding(explicit, derived_config.window_padding_balance);
557	        } else {
558	            size.padding = explicit;
559	        }
560	
561	        break :size size;
562	    };
563	
564	    // Initialize the render device if it hasn't been initialized already
565	    const render_device = app.device orelse device: {
566	        const device_options = try rt_surface.renderDeviceOptions();
567	        const device: *rendererpkg.Device = try .init(alloc, device_options);
568	        app.device = device;
569	        break :device device;
570	    };
571	
572	    // Create our terminal grid with the initial size
573	    const app_mailbox: App.Mailbox = .{ .rt_app = rt_app, .mailbox = &app.mailbox };
574	    var renderer_impl = renderer: {
575	        // Renderer.init takes ownership of the config only on success, after
576	        // which the errdefer below frees it, so this errdefer must end here.
577	        var renderer_config = try Renderer.DerivedConfig.init(alloc, config);
578	        errdefer renderer_config.deinit();
579	
580	        break :renderer try Renderer.init(alloc, .{
581	            .device = render_device,
582	            .config = renderer_config,
583	            .font_grid = font_grid,
584	            .size = size,
585	            .surface_mailbox = .{ .surface = self, .app = app_mailbox },
586	            .rt_surface = rt_surface,
587	            .thread = &self.renderer_thread,
588	        });
589	    };
590	    errdefer renderer_impl.deinit();
591	
592	    // The mutex used to protect our renderer state.
593	    const mutex = try alloc.create(std.Io.Mutex);
594	    mutex.* = .init;
595	    errdefer alloc.destroy(mutex);
596	
597	    // Create the renderer thread
598	    var render_thread = try rendererpkg.Thread.init(
599	        alloc,
600	        config,
601	        rt_surface,
602	        &self.renderer,
603	        &self.renderer_state,
604	    );
605	    errdefer render_thread.deinit();
606	
607	    // Create the IO thread
608	    var io_thread = try termio.Thread.init(alloc);
609	    errdefer io_thread.deinit();
610	
611	    self.* = .{
612	        .id = id: {
613	            while (true) {
614	                const candidate = candidate: {
615	                    const rng_impl: std.Random.IoSource = .{ .io = global.io() };
616	                    const rng = rng_impl.interface();
617	                    break :candidate rng.int(u64);
618	                };
619	                if (candidate == 0) continue;
620	                break :id candidate;
621	            }
622	        },
623	        .alloc = alloc,
624	        .app = app,
625	        .rt_app = rt_app,
626	        .rt_surface = rt_surface,
627	        .font_grid_key = font_grid_key,
628	        .font_size = font_size,
629	        .font_size_adjusted = false,
630	        .font_metrics = font_grid.metrics,
631	        .renderer = renderer_impl,
632	        .renderer_thread = render_thread,
633	        .renderer_state = .{
634	            .mutex = mutex,
635	            .terminal = &self.io.terminal,
636	        },
637	        .renderer_thr = undefined,
638	        .mouse = .{},
639	        .keyboard = .{},
640	        .io = undefined,
641	        .io_thread = io_thread,
642	        .io_thr = undefined,
643	        .size = size,
644	        .config = derived_config,
645	
646	        // Our conditional state is initialized to the app state. This
647	        // lets us get the most likely correct color theme and so on.
648	        .config_conditional_state = app.config_conditional_state,
649	    };
650	
651	    // The command we're going to execute
652	    const command: ?configpkg.Command = command: {
653	        if (app.first) {
654	            if (config.@"initial-command") |command| {
655	                break :command command;
656	            }
657	        }
658	        break :command config.command;
659	    };
660	
661	    // Start our IO implementation
662	    // This separate block ({}) is important because our errdefers must
663	    // be scoped here to be valid.
664	    {
665	        // Exec.init takes ownership of the env even if it fails, so the
666	        // errdefer for it must not reach past the call.
667	        const env = termio_env: {
668	            var env = rt_surface.defaultTermioEnv() catch |err| env: {
669	                // If an error occurs, we don't want to block surface startup.
670	                log.warn("error getting env map for surface err={}", .{err});
671	                break :env global.environMap() catch std.process.Environ.Map.init(alloc);
672	            };
673	            errdefer env.deinit();
674	
675	            // don't leak GHOSTTY_LOG to any subprocesses
676	            _ = env.orderedRemove("GHOSTTY_LOG");
677	
678	            var buf: [18]u8 = undefined;
679	            try env.put(
680	                "GHOSTTY_SURFACE_ID",
681	                std.mem.print(&buf, "0x{x:0>16}", .{self.id}) catch unreachable,
682	            );
683	
684	            break :termio_env env;
685	        };
686	
687	        // Initialize our IO backend
688	        var io_exec = try termio.Exec.init(alloc, .{
689	            .command = command,
690	            .env = env,
691	            .env_override = config.env,
692	            .shell_integration = config.@"shell-integration",
693	            .shell_integration_features = config.@"shell-integration-features",
694	            .cursor_blink = config.@"cursor-style-blink",
695	            .working_directory = if (config.@"working-directory") |wd| wd.value() else null,
696	            .resources_dir = global.resourcesDir().host(),
697	            .term = config.term,
698	            .rt_pre_exec_info = .init(config),
699	            .rt_post_fork_info = .init(config),
700	        });
701	        errdefer io_exec.deinit();
702	
703	        // Initialize our IO mailbox
704	        var io_mailbox = try termio.Mailbox.initSPSC(alloc);
705	        errdefer io_mailbox.deinit(alloc);
706	
707	        // Termio.init takes ownership of the config only on success.
708	        var io_config = try termio.Termio.DerivedConfig.init(alloc, config);
709	        errdefer io_config.deinit();
710	
711	        try termio.Termio.init(&self.io, alloc, .{
712	            .size = size,
713	            .full_config = config,
714	            .config = io_config,
715	            .backend = .{ .exec = io_exec },
716	            .mailbox = io_mailbox,
717	            .renderer_state = &self.renderer_state,
718	            .renderer_wakeup = render_thread.wakeup,
719	            .renderer_mailbox = render_thread.mailbox,
720	            .surface_mailbox = .{ .surface = self, .app = app_mailbox },
721	        });
722	    }
723	    // Outside the block, IO has now taken ownership of our temporary state
724	    // so we can just defer this and not the subcomponents.
725	    errdefer self.io.deinit();
726	
727	    // Report initial cell size on surface creation
728	    _ = try rt_app.performAction(
729	        .{ .surface = self },
730	        .cell_size,
731	        .{ .width = size.cell.width, .height = size.cell.height },
732	    );
733	
734	    _ = try rt_app.performAction(
735	        .{ .surface = self },
736	        .size_limit,
737	        .{
738	            .min_width = size.cell.width * min_window_width_cells,
739	            .min_height = size.cell.height * min_window_height_cells,
740	            // No max:
741	            .max_width = 0,
742	            .max_height = 0,
743	        },
744	    );
745	
746	    // Call our size callback which handles all our retina setup
747	    // Note: this shouldn't be necessary and when we clean up the surface
748	    // init stuff we should get rid of this. But this is required because
749	    // sizeCallback does retina-aware stuff we don't do here and don't want
750	    // to duplicate.
751	    try self.resize(self.size.screen);
752	
753	    // Start our renderer thread
754	    self.renderer_thr = try std.Thread.spawn(
755	        .{},
756	        rendererpkg.Thread.threadMain,
757	        .{&self.renderer_thread},
758	    );
759	    self.renderer_thr.setName(global.io(), "renderer") catch {};
760	
761	    // Start our IO thread
762	    self.io_thr = try std.Thread.spawn(
763	        .{},
764	        termio.Thread.threadMain,
765	        .{ &self.io_thread, &self.io },
766	    );
767	    self.io_thr.setName(global.io(), "io") catch {};
768	
769	    // Determine our initial window size if configured. We need to do this
770	    // quite late in the process because our height/width are in grid dimensions,
771	    // so we need to know our cell sizes first.
772	    //
773	    // Note: it is important to do this after the renderer is setup above.
774	    // This allows the apprt to fully initialize the surface before we
775	    // start messing with the window.
776	    self.recomputeInitialSize() catch |err| {
777	        // We don't treat this as a fatal error because not setting
778	        // an initial size shouldn't stop our terminal from working.
779	        log.warn("unable to set initial window size: {}", .{err});
780	    };
781	
782	    if (config.title) |title| {
783	        _ = try rt_app.performAction(
784	            .{ .surface = self },
785	            .set_title,
786	            .{ .title = title },
787	        );
788	    } else if ((comptime builtin.target.os.tag == .linux) and
789	        config.@"_xdg-terminal-exec")
790	    xdg: {
791	        // For xdg-terminal-exec execution we special-case and set the window
792	        // title to the command being executed. This allows window managers
793	        // to set custom styling based on the command being executed.
794	        const v = command orelse break :xdg;
795	        const title = v.string(alloc) catch |err| {
796	            log.warn(
797	                "error copying command for title, title will not be set err={}",
798	                .{err},
799	            );
800	            break :xdg;
801	        };
802	        defer alloc.free(title);
803	        _ = try rt_app.performAction(
804	            .{ .surface = self },
805	            .set_title,
806	            .{ .title = title },
807	        );
808	    } else if (command) |cmd| switch (cmd) {
809	        // If a user specifies a command it is appropriate to set the title as argv[0]
810	        // we know in the case of a direct command it has been supplied by the user
811	        .direct => |cmd_str| if (cmd_str.len != 0) {
812	            _ = try rt_app.performAction(
813	                .{ .surface = self },
814	                .set_title,
815	                .{ .title = cmd_str[0] },
816	            );
817	        },
818	
819	        // We won't set the title in the case the shell expands the command
820	        // as that should typically be used to launch a shell which should
821	        // set its own titles
822	        .shell => {},
823	    };
824	
825	    // We are no longer the first surface
826	    app.first = false;
827	}
829	pub fn deinit(self: *Surface) void {
830	    // Stop search thread
831	    if (self.search) |*s| s.deinit();
832	
833	    // Stop rendering thread
834	    {
835	        self.renderer_thread.stop.notify() catch |err|
836	            log.err("error notifying renderer thread to stop, may stall err={}", .{err});
837	        self.renderer_thr.join();
838	    }
839	
840	    // Stop our IO thread
841	    {
842	        self.io_thread.stop.notify() catch |err|
843	            log.err("error notifying io thread to stop, may stall err={}", .{err});
844	        self.io_thr.join();
845	    }
846	
847	    // We need to deinit AFTER everything is stopped, since there are
848	    // shared values between the two threads.
849	    //
850	    // The renderer must be deinitialized before the renderer thread:
851	    // on macOS the renderer's display link callback points at the
852	    // thread's `draw_now` handle, and the renderer's deinit is what
853	    // stops the display link for good.
854	    self.renderer.deinit();
855	    self.renderer_thread.deinit();
856	    self.io_thread.deinit();
857	    self.mouse.selection_gesture.deinit(&self.io.terminal);
858	    self.io.deinit();
859	
860	    if (self.inspector) |v| {
861	        v.deinit(self.alloc);
862	        self.alloc.destroy(v);
863	    }
864	
865	    // Clean up our keyboard state
866	    for (self.keyboard.sequence_queued.items) |req| req.deinit();
867	    self.keyboard.sequence_queued.deinit(self.alloc);
868	    self.keyboard.table_stack.deinit(self.alloc);
869	
870	    // Clean up our font grid
871	    self.app.font_grid_set.deref(self.font_grid_key);
872	
873	    // Clean up our render state
874	    if (self.renderer_state.preedit) |p| self.alloc.free(p.codepoints);
875	    self.alloc.destroy(self.renderer_state.mutex);
876	    self.config.deinit();
877	
878	    log.info("surface closed id={x}", .{self.id});
879	}
881	/// Close this surface. This will trigger the runtime to start the
882	/// close process, which should ultimately deinitialize this surface.
883	pub fn close(self: *Surface) void {
884	    self.rt_surface.close(self.needsConfirmQuit());
885	}
886	
887	/// Returns a mailbox that can be used to send messages to this surface.
888	inline fn surfaceMailbox(self: *Surface) Mailbox {
889	    return .{
890	        .surface = self,
891	        .app = .{ .rt_app = self.rt_app, .mailbox = &self.app.mailbox },
892	    };
893	}
895	/// Queue a message for the IO thread, taking ownership of `msg`.
896	///
897	/// We centralize all our logic into this spot so we can intercept
898	/// messages for example in readonly mode.
899	fn queueIo(
900	    self: *Surface,
901	    msg: termio.Message,
902	    mutex: termio.Termio.MutexState,
903	) void {
904	    // In readonly mode, we don't allow any writes through to the pty.
905	    if (self.readonly) {
906	        switch (msg) {
907	            .write_small,
908	            .write_stable,
909	            .write_alloc,
910	            => {
911	                msg.deinit();
912	                return;
913	            },
914	
915	            else => {},
916	        }
917	    }
918	
919	    self.io.queueMessage(msg, mutex);
920	}
925	pub fn draw(self: *Surface) !void {
926	    // Renderers are required to support `drawFrame` being called from
927	    // the main thread, so that they can update contents during resize.
928	    try self.renderer.drawFrame(true);
929	}
931	/// Activate the inspector. This will begin collecting inspection data.
932	/// This will not affect the GUI. The GUI must use performAction to
933	/// show/hide the inspector UI.
934	pub fn activateInspector(self: *Surface) !void {
935	    if (self.inspector != null) return;
936	
937	    // Setup the inspector
938	    const ptr = try self.alloc.create(inspectorpkg.Inspector);
939	    errdefer self.alloc.destroy(ptr);
940	    ptr.* = try inspectorpkg.Inspector.init(self.alloc);
941	    errdefer ptr.deinit(self.alloc);
942	    self.inspector = ptr;
943	    errdefer self.inspector = null;
944	
945	    // Put the inspector onto the render state
946	    {
947	        self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
948	        defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
949	        assert(self.renderer_state.inspector == null);
950	        self.renderer_state.inspector = self.inspector;
951	    }
952	
953	    // Notify our components we have an inspector active
954	    _ = self.renderer_thread.mailbox.push(global.io(), .{ .inspector = true }, .{ .forever = {} });
955	    self.queueIo(.{ .inspector = true }, .unlocked);
956	}
971	/// Deactivate the inspector and stop collecting any information.
972	pub fn deactivateInspector(self: *Surface) void {
973	    const insp = self.inspector orelse return;
974	
975	    // Remove the inspector from the render state
976	    {
977	        self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
978	        defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
979	        assert(self.renderer_state.inspector != null);
980	        self.renderer_state.inspector = null;
981	    }
982	
983	    // Notify our components we have deactivated inspector
984	    _ = self.renderer_thread.mailbox.push(global.io(), .{ .inspector = false }, .{ .forever = {} });
985	    self.queueIo(.{ .inspector = false }, .unlocked);
986	
987	    // Deinit the inspector
988	    insp.deinit(self.alloc);
989	    self.alloc.destroy(insp);
990	    self.inspector = null;
991	}
995	pub fn needsConfirmQuit(self: *Surface) bool {
996	    // If the surface is in read-only mode, always require confirmation
997	    if (self.readonly) return true;
998	
999	    // If the child has exited, then our process is certainly not alive.
1000	    // We check this first to avoid the locking overhead below.
1001	    if (self.child_exited) return false;
1002	
1003	    // Check the configuration for confirming close behavior.
1004	    return switch (self.config.confirm_close_surface) {
1005	        .always => true,
1006	        .false => false,
1007	        .true => true: {
1008	            self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
1009	            defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
1010	            break :true !self.io.terminal.cursorIsAtPrompt();
1011	        },
1012	    };
1013	}
1015	/// Called from the app thread to handle mailbox messages to our specific
1016	/// surface.
1017	pub fn handleMessage(self: *Surface, msg: Message) !void {
1018	    switch (msg) {
1019	        .change_config => |config| try self.updateConfig(config),
1020	
1021	        .set_title => |*v| {
1022	            // We ignore the message in case the title was set via config.
1023	            if (self.config.title != null) {
1024	                log.debug("ignoring title change request since static title is set via config", .{});
1025	                return;
1026	            }
1027	
1028	            // The ptrCast just gets sliceTo to return the proper type.
1029	            // We know that our title should end in 0.
1030	            const slice = std.mem.sliceTo(@as([*:0]const u8, @ptrCast(v)), 0);
1031	            log.debug("changing title \"{s}\"", .{slice});
1032	            _ = try self.rt_app.performAction(
1033	                .{ .surface = self },
1034	                .set_title,
1035	                .{ .title = slice },
1036	            );
1037	        },
1038	
1039	        .report_title => |style| report_title: {
1040	            if (!self.config.title_report) {
1041	                log.info("report_title requested, but disabled via config", .{});
1042	                break :report_title;
1043	            }
1044	
1045	            const title: ?[:0]const u8 = self.rt_surface.getTitle();
1046	            const data = switch (style) {
1047	                .csi_21_t => try self.alloc.print(
1048	                    "\x1b]l{s}\x1b\\",
1049	                    .{title orelse ""},
1050	                ),
1051	            };
1052	
1053	            // We always use an allocating message because we don't know
1054	            // the length of the title and this isn't a performance critical
1055	            // path.
1056	            self.queueIo(.{
1057	                .write_alloc = .{
1058	                    .alloc = self.alloc,
1059	                    .data = data,
1060	                },
1061	            }, .unlocked);
1062	        },
1063	
1064	        .resize_window => |size| try self.resizeWindow(size),
1065	
1066	        .color_change => |change| color_change: {
1067	            // Notify our apprt, but don't send a mode 2031 DSR report
1068	            // because VT sequences were used to change the color.
1069	            _ = try self.rt_app.performAction(
1070	                .{ .surface = self },
1071	                .color_change,
1072	                .{
1073	                    .kind = switch (change.target) {
1074	                        .palette => |v| @fromBackingInt(v),
1075	                        .dynamic => |dyn| switch (dyn) {
1076	                            .foreground => .foreground,
1077	                            .background => .background,
1078	                            .cursor => .cursor,
1079	                            // Unsupported dynamic color change notification type
1080	                            else => break :color_change,
1081	                        },
1082	                        // Special colors aren't supported for change notification
1083	                        .special => break :color_change,
1084	                    },
1085	                    .r = change.color.r,
1086	                    .g = change.color.g,
1087	                    .b = change.color.b,
1088	                },
1089	            );
1090	        },
1091	
1092	        .set_mouse_shape => |shape| {
1093	            log.debug("changing mouse shape: {}", .{shape});
1094	            _ = try self.rt_app.performAction(
1095	                .{ .surface = self },
1096	                .mouse_shape,
1097	                shape,
1098	            );
1099	        },
1100	
1101	        .clipboard_read => |clipboard| {
1102	            if (self.config.clipboard_read == .deny) {
1103	                log.info("application attempted to read clipboard, but 'clipboard-read' is set to deny", .{});
1104	                return;
1105	            }
1106	
1107	            _ = try self.startClipboardRequest(.standard, .{ .osc_52_read = clipboard });
1108	        },
1109	
1110	        .kitty_clipboard_read => |req| try self.kittyClipboardRead(req),
1111	
1112	        .kitty_clipboard_write => |req| try self.kittyClipboardWrite(req),
1113	
1114	        .clipboard_write => |w| switch (w.req) {
1115	            .small => |v| try self.clipboardWrite(v.data[0..v.len], w.clipboard_type),
1116	            .stable => |v| try self.clipboardWrite(v, w.clipboard_type),
1117	            .alloc => |v| {
1118	                defer v.alloc.free(v.data);
1119	                try self.clipboardWrite(v.data, w.clipboard_type);
1120	            },
1121	        },
1122	
1123	        .pwd_change => |w| {
1124	            defer w.deinit();
1125	
1126	            var stack_buf: [256]u8 = undefined;
1127	            var stack: std.heap.BufferFirstAllocator = .init(&stack_buf, self.alloc);
1128	            const alloc = stack.allocator();
1129	            const str = try alloc.dupeSentinel(u8, w.slice(), 0);
1130	            defer alloc.free(str);
1131	
1132	            _ = try self.rt_app.performAction(
1133	                .{ .surface = self },
1134	                .pwd,
1135	                .{ .pwd = str },
1136	            );
1137	        },
1138	
1139	        .close => self.close(),
1140	
1141	        .child_exited => |v| self.childExited(v),
1142	
1143	        .desktop_notification => |notification| {
1144	            if (!self.config.desktop_notifications) {
1145	                log.info("application attempted to display a desktop notification, but 'desktop-notifications' is disabled", .{});
1146	                return;
1147	            }
1148	
1149	            const title = std.mem.sliceTo(&notification.title, 0);
1150	            const body = std.mem.sliceTo(&notification.body, 0);
1151	            try self.showDesktopNotification(title, body);
1152	        },
1153	
1154	        .redraw => self.redraw(),
1155	
1156	        .renderer_health => |health| self.updateRendererHealth(health),
1157	
1158	        .scrollbar => |scrollbar| self.updateScrollbar(scrollbar),
1159	
1160	        .present_surface => try self.presentSurface(),
1161	
1162	        .password_input => |v| try self.passwordInput(v),
1163	
1164	        .ring_bell => bell: {
1165	            const now: std.Io.Timestamp = .now(global.io(), .awake);
1166	            if (self.last_bell_time) |last| {
1167	                if (last.durationTo(now).toMilliseconds() < 100) break :bell;
1168	            }
1169	            self.last_bell_time = now;
1170	            _ = self.rt_app.performAction(
1171	                .{ .surface = self },
1172	                .ring_bell,
1173	                {},
1174	            ) catch |err| {
1175	                log.warn("apprt failed to ring bell={}", .{err});
1176	            };
1177	        },
1178	
1179	        .progress_report => |v| {
1180	            _ = self.rt_app.performAction(
1181	                .{ .surface = self },
1182	                .progress_report,
1183	                v,
1184	            ) catch |err| {
1185	                log.warn("apprt failed to report progress err={}", .{err});
1186	            };
1187	        },
1188	
1189	        .selection_scroll_tick => |active| {
1190	            self.selection_scroll_active = active;
1191	            try self.selectionScrollTick();
1192	        },
1193	
1194	        .start_command => {
1195	            self.command_timer = .now(global.io(), .awake);
1196	        },
1197	
1198	        .stop_command => |v| timer: {
1199	            const end: std.Io.Timestamp = .now(global.io(), .awake);
1200	            const start = self.command_timer orelse break :timer;
1201	            self.command_timer = null;
1202	            const duration_raw = start.durationTo(end).nanoseconds;
1203	            assert(duration_raw >= 0 and duration_raw <= std.math.maxInt(u64));
1204	
1205	            const duration: Duration = .{
1206	                .duration = @as(
1207	                    u64,
1208	                    @intCast(std.math.clamp(start.durationTo(end).nanoseconds, 0, std.math.maxInt(u64))),
1209	                ),
1210	            };
1211	            log.debug("command took {f}", .{duration});
1212	
1213	            _ = self.rt_app.performAction(
1214	                .{ .surface = self },
1215	                .command_finished,
1216	                .{
1217	                    .exit_code = v,
1218	                    .duration = duration,
1219	                },
1220	            ) catch |err| {
1221	                log.warn("apprt failed to notify command finish={}", .{err});
1222	            };
1223	        },
1224	
1225	        .search_total => |v| {
1226	            _ = try self.rt_app.performAction(
1227	                .{ .surface = self },
1228	                .search_total,
1229	                .{ .total = v },
1230	            );
1231	        },
1232	
1233	        .search_selected => |v| {
1234	            _ = try self.rt_app.performAction(
1235	                .{ .surface = self },
1236	                .search_selected,
1237	                .{ .selected = v },
1238	            );
1239	        },
1240	    }
1241	}
1297	fn childExited(self: *Surface, info: apprt.surface.Message.ChildExited) void {
1298	    // Mark our flag that we exited immediately
1299	    self.child_exited = true;
1300	
1301	    // If our runtime was below some threshold then we assume that this
1302	    // was an abnormal exit and we show an error message.
1303	    if (info.runtime_ms <= self.config.abnormal_command_exit_runtime_ms) runtime: {
1304	        // On macOS, our exit code detection doesn't work, possibly
1305	        // because of our `login` wrapper. More investigation required.
1306	        if (comptime !builtin.target.os.tag.isDarwin()) {
1307	            // If the exit code is 0 then it was a good exit.
1308	            if (info.exit_code == 0) break :runtime;
1309	        }
1310	
1311	        log.warn("abnormal process exit detected, showing error message", .{});
1312	
1313	        // Try and show a GUI message. If it returns true, don't do anything else.
1314	        if (self.rt_app.performAction(
1315	            .{ .surface = self },
1316	            .show_child_exited,
1317	            info,
1318	        ) catch |err| gui: {
1319	            log.err("error trying to show native child exited GUI err={}", .{err});
1320	            break :gui false;
1321	        }) return;
1322	
1323	        // If a native GUI notification was not shown, update our terminal to
1324	        // note the abnormal exit.
1325	        self.childExitedAbnormally(info) catch |err| {
1326	            log.err("error handling abnormal child exit err={}", .{err});
1327	            return;
1328	        };
1329	
1330	        return;
1331	    }
1332	
1333	    // We output a message so that the user knows what's going on and
1334	    // doesn't think their terminal just froze. We show this unconditionally
1335	    // on close even if `wait_after_command` is false and the surface closes
1336	    // immediately because if a user does an `undo` to restore a closed
1337	    // surface then they will see this message and know the process has
1338	    // completed.
1339	    terminal: {
1340	        // First try and show a native GUI message.
1341	        if (self.rt_app.performAction(
1342	            .{ .surface = self },
1343	            .show_child_exited,
1344	            info,
1345	        ) catch |err| gui: {
1346	            log.err("error trying to show native child exited GUI err={}", .{err});
1347	            break :gui false;
1348	        }) break :terminal;
1349	
1350	        // If the native GUI can't be shown, display a text message in the
1351	        // terminal.
1352	        self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
1353	        defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
1354	        const t: *terminal.Terminal = self.renderer_state.terminal;
1355	        t.carriageReturn();
1356	        t.linefeed() catch break :terminal;
1357	        t.printString("Process exited. Press any key to close the terminal.") catch
1358	            break :terminal;
1359	        t.modes.set(.cursor_visible, false);
1360	
1361	        // We also want to ensure that normal keyboard encoding is on
1362	        // so that we can close the terminal. We close the terminal on
1363	        // any key press that encodes a character.
1364	        t.modes.set(.disable_keyboard, false);
1365	        t.screens.active.kitty_keyboard.set(.set, .disabled);
1366	    }
1367	
1368	    // Waiting after command we stop here. The terminal is updated, our
1369	    // state is updated, and now its up to the user to decide what to do.
1370	    if (self.config.wait_after_command) return;
1371	
1372	    // If we aren't waiting after the command, then we exit immediately
1373	    // with no confirmation.
1374	    self.close();
1375	}
1377	/// Called when the child process exited abnormally.
1378	fn childExitedAbnormally(
1379	    self: *Surface,
1380	    info: apprt.surface.Message.ChildExited,
1381	) !void {
1382	    var arena = ArenaAllocator.init(self.alloc);
1383	    defer arena.deinit();
1384	    const alloc = arena.allocator();
1385	
1386	    // Build up our command for the error message
1387	    const command = try std.mem.join(alloc, " ", switch (self.io.backend) {
1388	        .exec => |*exec| exec.subprocess.args,
1389	    });
1390	    const runtime_str = try alloc.print("{d} ms", .{info.runtime_ms});
1391	
1392	    self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
1393	    defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
1394	    const t: *terminal.Terminal = self.renderer_state.terminal;
1395	
1396	    // No matter what move the cursor back to the column 0.
1397	    t.carriageReturn();
1398	
1399	    // Reset styles
1400	    try t.setAttribute(.{ .unset = {} });
1401	
1402	    // If there is data in the viewport, we want to scroll down
1403	    // a little bit and write a horizontal rule before writing
1404	    // our message. This lets the use see the error message the
1405	    // command may have output.
1406	    const viewport_str = try t.plainString(alloc);
1407	    if (viewport_str.len > 0) {
1408	        try t.linefeed();
1409	        for (0..t.cols) |_| try t.print(0x2501);
1410	        t.carriageReturn();
1411	        try t.linefeed();
1412	        try t.linefeed();
1413	    }
1414	
1415	    // Output our error message
1416	    try t.setAttribute(.{ .@"8_fg" = .bright_red });
1417	    try t.setAttribute(.{ .bold = {} });
1418	    try t.printString("Ghostty failed to launch the requested command:");
1419	    try t.setAttribute(.{ .unset = {} });
1420	
1421	    t.carriageReturn();
1422	    try t.linefeed();
1423	    try t.linefeed();
1424	    try t.printString(command);
1425	    try t.setAttribute(.{ .unset = {} });
1426	
1427	    t.carriageReturn();
1428	    try t.linefeed();
1429	    try t.linefeed();
1430	    try t.printString("Runtime: ");
1431	    try t.setAttribute(.{ .@"8_fg" = .red });
1432	    try t.printString(runtime_str);
1433	    try t.setAttribute(.{ .unset = {} });
1434	
1435	    // We don't print this on macOS because the exit code is always 0
1436	    // due to the way we launch the process.
1437	    if (comptime !builtin.target.os.tag.isDarwin()) {
1438	        const exit_code_str = try alloc.print("{d}", .{info.exit_code});
1439	        t.carriageReturn();
1440	        try t.linefeed();
1441	        try t.printString("Exit Code: ");
1442	        try t.setAttribute(.{ .@"8_fg" = .red });
1443	        try t.printString(exit_code_str);
1444	        try t.setAttribute(.{ .unset = {} });
1445	    }
1446	
1447	    t.carriageReturn();
1448	    try t.linefeed();
1449	    try t.linefeed();
1450	    try t.printString("Press any key to close the window.");
1451	
1452	    // Hide the cursor
1453	    t.modes.set(.cursor_visible, false);
1454	}
1485	fn searchCallback(event: terminal.search.Thread.Event, ud: ?*anyopaque) void {
1486	    // IMPORTANT: This function is run on the SEARCH THREAD! It is NOT SAFE
1487	    // to access anything other than values that never change on the surface.
1488	    // The surface is guaranteed to be valid for the lifetime of the search
1489	    // thread.
1490	    const self: *Surface = @ptrCast(@alignCast(ud.?));
1491	    self.searchCallback_(event) catch |err| {
1492	        log.warn("error in search callback err={}", .{err});
1493	    };
1494	}
1495	
1496	fn searchCallback_(
1497	    self: *Surface,
1498	    event: terminal.search.Thread.Event,
1499	) !void {
1500	    // NOTE: This runs on the search thread.
1501	
1502	    switch (event) {
1503	        .viewport_matches => |matches_unowned| {
1504	            var arena: ArenaAllocator = .init(self.alloc);
1505	            errdefer arena.deinit();
1506	            const alloc = arena.allocator();
1507	
1508	            const matches = try alloc.dupe(terminal.highlight.Flattened, matches_unowned);
1509	            for (matches) |*m| m.* = try m.clone(alloc);
1510	
1511	            _ = self.renderer_thread.mailbox.push(
1512	                global.io(),
1513	                .{ .search_viewport_matches = .{
1514	                    .arena = arena,
1515	                    .matches = matches,
1516	                } },
1517	                .forever,
1518	            );
1519	            try self.renderer_thread.wakeup.notify();
1520	        },
1521	
1522	        .selected_match => |selected_| {
1523	            if (selected_) |sel| {
1524	                // Copy the flattened match.
1525	                var arena: ArenaAllocator = .init(self.alloc);
1526	                errdefer arena.deinit();
1527	                const alloc = arena.allocator();
1528	                const match = try sel.highlight.clone(alloc);
1529	
1530	                _ = self.renderer_thread.mailbox.push(
1531	                    global.io(),
1532	                    .{ .search_selected_match = .{
1533	                        .arena = arena,
1534	                        .match = match,
1535	                    } },
1536	                    .forever,
1537	                );
1538	
1539	                // Send the selected index to the surface mailbox
1540	                _ = self.surfaceMailbox().push(
1541	                    .{ .search_selected = sel.idx },
1542	                    .forever,
1543	                );
1544	            } else {
1545	                // Reset our selected match
1546	                _ = self.renderer_thread.mailbox.push(
1547	                    global.io(),
1548	                    .{ .search_selected_match = null },
1549	                    .forever,
1550	                );
1551	
1552	                // Reset the selected index
1553	                _ = self.surfaceMailbox().push(
1554	                    .{ .search_selected = null },
1555	                    .forever,
1556	                );
1557	            }
1558	
1559	            try self.renderer_thread.wakeup.notify();
1560	        },
1561	
1562	        .total_matches => |total| {
1563	            _ = self.surfaceMailbox().push(
1564	                .{ .search_total = total },
1565	                .forever,
1566	            );
1567	        },
1568	
1569	        // When we quit, tell our renderer to reset any search state.
1570	        .quit => {
1571	            _ = self.renderer_thread.mailbox.push(
1572	                global.io(),
1573	                .{ .search_selected_match = null },
1574	                .forever,
1575	            );
1576	            _ = self.renderer_thread.mailbox.push(
1577	                global.io(),
1578	                .{ .search_viewport_matches = .{
1579	                    .arena = .init(self.alloc),
1580	                    .matches = &.{},
1581	                } },
1582	                .forever,
1583	            );
1584	            try self.renderer_thread.wakeup.notify();
1585	
1586	            // Reset search totals in the surface
1587	            _ = self.surfaceMailbox().push(
1588	                .{ .search_total = null },
1589	                .forever,
1590	            );
1591	            _ = self.surfaceMailbox().push(
1592	                .{ .search_selected = null },
1593	                .forever,
1594	            );
1595	        },
1596	
1597	        // Unhandled, so far.
1598	        .complete => {},
1599	    }
1600	}
1609	fn modsChanged(self: *Surface, mods: input.Mods) void {
1610	    // The only place we keep track of mods currently is on the mouse.
1611	    if (!self.mouse.mods.equal(mods)) {
1612	        // The mouse mods only contain binding modifiers since we don't
1613	        // want caps/num lock or sided modifiers to affect the mouse.
1614	        self.mouse.mods = mods.binding();
1615	
1616	        // We also need to update the renderer so it knows if it should
1617	        // highlight links. Additionally, mark the screen as dirty so
1618	        // that the highlight state of all links is properly updated.
1619	        {
1620	            self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
1621	            defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
1622	            self.renderer_state.mouse.mods = self.mouseModsWithCapture(self.mouse.mods);
1623	
1624	            // We use the clear screen dirty flag to force a rebuild of all
1625	            // rows because changing mouse mods can affect the highlight state
1626	            // of a link. If there is no link this seems very wasteful but
1627	            // its really only one frame so it's not so bad.
1628	            self.renderer_state.terminal.flags.dirty.clear = true;
1629	        }
1630	
1631	        self.queueRender() catch |err| {
1632	            // Not a big deal if this fails.
1633	            log.warn("failed to notify renderer of mods change err={}", .{err});
1634	        };
1635	    }
1636	}
1804	pub fn updateConfig(
1805	    self: *Surface,
1806	    original: *const configpkg.Config,
1807	) !void {
1808	    // Apply our conditional state. If we fail to apply the conditional state
1809	    // then we log and attempt to move forward with the old config.
1810	    var config_: ?configpkg.Config = original.changeConditionalState(
1811	        self.config_conditional_state,
1812	    ) catch |err| err: {
1813	        log.warn("failed to apply conditional state to config err={}", .{err});
1814	        break :err null;
1815	    };
1816	    defer if (config_) |*c| c.deinit();
1817	
1818	    // We want a config pointer for everything so we get that either
1819	    // based on our conditional state or the original config.
1820	    const config: *const configpkg.Config = if (config_) |*c| c else original;
1821	
1822	    // Update our new derived config immediately
1823	    const derived = DerivedConfig.init(self.alloc, config) catch |err| {
1824	        // If the derivation fails then we just log and return. We don't
1825	        // hard fail in this case because we don't want to error the surface
1826	        // when config fails we just want to keep using the old config.
1827	        log.err("error updating configuration err={}", .{err});
1828	        return;
1829	    };
1830	    self.config.deinit();
1831	    self.config = derived;
1832	
1833	    // If our mouse is hidden but we disabled mouse hiding, then show it again.
1834	    if (!self.config.mouse_hide_while_typing and self.mouse.hidden) {
1835	        self.showMouse();
1836	    }
1837	
1838	    // If we are in the middle of a key sequence, clear it.
1839	    self.endKeySequence(.drop, .free);
1840	
1841	    // Deactivate all key tables since they may have changed. Importantly,
1842	    // we store pointers into the config as part of our table stack so
1843	    // we can't keep them active across config changes. But this behavior
1844	    // also matches key sequences.
1845	    _ = self.deactivateAllKeyTables() catch |err| {
1846	        log.warn("failed to deactivate key tables err={}", .{err});
1847	    };
1848	
1849	    // Before sending any other config changes, we give the renderer a new font
1850	    // grid. We could check to see if there was an actual change to the font,
1851	    // but this is easier and pretty rare so it's not a performance concern.
1852	    //
1853	    // (Calling setFontSize builds and sends a new font grid to the renderer.)
1854	    try self.setFontSize(font_size: {
1855	        // If we have manually adjusted the font size, keep it that way.
1856	        if (self.font_size_adjusted) {
1857	            log.info("font size manually adjusted, preserving previous size on config reload", .{});
1858	            break :font_size self.font_size;
1859	        }
1860	
1861	        // If we haven't, then we update to the configured font size.
1862	        // This allows config changes to update the font size. We used to
1863	        // never do this but it was a common source of confusion and people
1864	        // assumed that Ghostty was broken! This logic makes more sense.
1865	        var size = self.font_size;
1866	        size.points = std.math.clamp(config.@"font-size", 1.0, 255.0);
1867	        break :font_size size;
1868	    });
1869	
1870	    // We need to store our configs in a heap-allocated pointer so that
1871	    // our messages aren't huge.
1872	    var renderer_message = try rendererpkg.Message.initChangeConfig(self.alloc, config);
1873	    errdefer renderer_message.deinit();
1874	    var termio_config_ptr = try self.alloc.create(termio.Termio.DerivedConfig);
1875	    errdefer self.alloc.destroy(termio_config_ptr);
1876	    termio_config_ptr.* = try termio.Termio.DerivedConfig.init(self.alloc, config);
1877	    errdefer termio_config_ptr.deinit();
1878	
1879	    _ = self.renderer_thread.mailbox.push(global.io(), renderer_message, .{ .forever = {} });
1880	    self.queueIo(.{
1881	        .change_config = .{
1882	            .alloc = self.alloc,
1883	            .ptr = termio_config_ptr,
1884	        },
1885	    }, .unlocked);
1886	
1887	    // With mailbox messages sent, we have to wake them up so they process it.
1888	    self.queueRender() catch |err| {
1889	        log.warn("failed to notify renderer of config change err={}", .{err});
1890	    };
1891	
1892	    // If we have a title set then we update our window to have the
1893	    // newly configured title.
1894	    if (config.title) |title| _ = try self.rt_app.performAction(
1895	        .{ .surface = self },
1896	        .set_title,
1897	        .{ .title = title },
1898	    );
1899	
1900	    // Notify the window
1901	    _ = try self.rt_app.performAction(
1902	        .{ .surface = self },
1903	        .config_change,
1904	        .{ .config = config },
1905	    );
1906	}
2450	/// This must be called with the renderer mutex held.
2451	fn setSelection(self: *Surface, sel_: ?terminal.Selection) !void {
2452	    // Compute the transition before `select` below, which untracks (frees)
2453	    // the previous selection's tracked pins; reading them after would be a
2454	    // use-after-free.
2455	    const prev_ = self.io.terminal.screens.active.selection;
2456	    const changed = changed: {
2457	        const prev = prev_ orelse break :changed sel_ != null;
2458	        const sel = sel_ orelse break :changed true;
2459	        break :changed !sel.eql(prev);
2460	    };
2461	
2462	    try self.io.terminal.screens.active.select(sel_);
2463	
2464	    if (changed) {
2465	        _ = self.rt_app.performAction(
2466	            .{ .surface = self },
2467	            .selection_changed,
2468	            {},
2469	        ) catch |err| {
2470	            log.warn("apprt failed selection_changed notification err={}", .{err});
2471	        };
2472	    }
2473	}
2474	
2475	/// Set a selection and, per `copy_on_select`, copy it to the clipboard.
2476	/// For committing selection gestures (mouse release, select-all binding).
2477	///
2478	/// This must be called with the renderer mutex held.
2479	fn setSelectionAndCopy(self: *Surface, sel: terminal.Selection) !void {
2480	    try self.setSelection(sel);
2481	
2482	    switch (self.config.copy_on_select) {
2483	        .none => {},
2484	
2485	        // The selection clipboard is set if supported, otherwise nothing is copied.
2486	        .primary => try self.copySelectionToClipboards(
2487	            sel,
2488	            &.{.selection},
2489	            .mixed,
2490	        ),
2491	
2492	        // Only the standard clipboard is set.
2493	        .clipboard => try self.copySelectionToClipboards(
2494	            sel,
2495	            &.{.standard},
2496	            .mixed,
2497	        ),
2498	
2499	        // Both standard and selection clipboards are set.
2500	        .both => try self.copySelectionToClipboards(
2501	            sel,
2502	            &.{ .standard, .selection },
2503	            .mixed,
2504	        ),
2505	    }
2506	}
2510	fn setCellSize(self: *Surface, size: rendererpkg.CellSize) !void {
2511	    // Update our cell size within our size struct
2512	    self.size.cell = size;
2513	    self.balancePaddingIfNeeded();
2514	
2515	    // Notify the terminal
2516	    self.queueIo(.{ .resize = self.size }, .unlocked);
2536	pub fn setFontSize(self: *Surface, size: font.face.DesiredSize) !void {
2537	    log.debug("set font size size={}", .{size.points});
2538	
2539	    // Update our font size so future changes work
2540	    self.font_size = size;
2541	
2542	    // We need to build up a new font stack for this font size.
2543	    const font_grid_key, const font_grid = try self.app.font_grid_set.ref(
2544	        &self.config.font,
2545	        self.font_size,
2546	    );
2547	    errdefer self.app.font_grid_set.deref(font_grid_key);
2548	
2549	    // Set our cell size
2550	    try self.setCellSize(.{
2551	        .width = font_grid.metrics.cell_width,
2552	        .height = font_grid.metrics.cell_height,
2553	    });
2554	
2555	    // Notify our render thread of the new font stack. The renderer
2556	    // MUST accept the new font grid and deref the old.
2557	    _ = self.renderer_thread.mailbox.push(global.io(), .{
2558	        .font_grid = .{
2559	            .grid = font_grid,
2560	            .set = &self.app.font_grid_set,
2561	            .old_key = self.font_grid_key,
2562	            .new_key = font_grid_key,
2563	        },
2564	    }, .{ .forever = {} });
2565	
2566	    // Once we've sent the key we can replace our key
2567	    self.font_grid_key = font_grid_key;
2568	    self.font_metrics = font_grid.metrics;
2569	
2570	    // Schedule render which also drains our mailbox
2571	    self.queueRender() catch unreachable;
2572	}
2573	
2574	/// This queues a render operation with the renderer thread. The render
2575	/// isn't guaranteed to happen immediately but it will happen as soon as
2576	/// practical.
2577	fn queueRender(self: *Surface) !void {
2578	    try self.renderer_thread.wakeup.notify();
2579	}
2600	pub fn sizeCallback(self: *Surface, size: apprt.SurfaceSize) !void {
2601	    // Crash metadata in case we crash in here
2602	    crash.sentry.thread_state = self.crashThreadState();
2603	    defer crash.sentry.thread_state = null;
2604	
2605	    const new_screen_size: rendererpkg.ScreenSize = .{
2606	        .width = size.width,
2607	        .height = size.height,
2608	    };
2609	
2610	    // Update our screen size, but only if it actually changed. And if
2611	    // the screen size didn't change, then our grid size could not have
2612	    // changed, so we just return.
2613	    if (self.size.screen.equals(new_screen_size)) return;
2614	
2615	    try self.resize(new_screen_size);
2616	}
2617	
2618	fn resize(self: *Surface, size: rendererpkg.ScreenSize) !void {
2619	    // Save our screen size
2620	    self.size.screen = size;
2621	    self.balancePaddingIfNeeded();
2622	
2623	    // Recalculate our grid size. Because Ghostty supports fluid resizing,
2624	    // its possible the grid doesn't change at all even if the screen size changes.
2625	    // We have to update the IO thread no matter what because we send
2626	    // pixel-level sizing to the subprocess.
2627	    const grid_size = self.size.grid();
2628	    if (grid_size.columns < 5 and (self.size.padding.left > 0 or self.size.padding.right > 0)) {
2629	        log.warn("WARNING: very small terminal grid detected with padding " ++
2630	            "set. Is your padding reasonable?", .{});
2631	    }
2632	    if (grid_size.rows < 2 and (self.size.padding.top > 0 or self.size.padding.bottom > 0)) {
2633	        log.warn("WARNING: very small terminal grid detected with padding " ++
2634	            "set. Is your padding reasonable?", .{});
2635	    }
2637	    // Mail the IO thread
2638	    self.queueIo(.{ .resize = self.size }, .unlocked);
2639	
2640	    // Mail the render thread so it updates its padding and screen size.
2641	    _ = self.renderer_thread.mailbox.push(
2642	        global.io(),
2643	        .{ .resize = self.size },
2644	        .forever,
2645	    );
2646	    self.queueRender() catch |err| {
2647	        log.warn("failed to notify renderer of resize err={}", .{err});
2648	    };
2649	}
2651	/// Recalculate the balanced padding if needed.
2652	fn balancePaddingIfNeeded(self: *Surface) void {
2653	    if (self.config.window_padding_balance == .false) return;
2654	    const content_scale = try self.rt_surface.getContentScale();
2655	    const x_dpi = content_scale.x * font.face.default_dpi;
2656	    const y_dpi = content_scale.y * font.face.default_dpi;
2657	    self.size.balancePadding(self.config.scaledPadding(x_dpi, y_dpi), self.config.window_padding_balance);
2658	}
2669	pub fn preeditCallback(self: *Surface, preedit_: ?[]const u8) !void {
2670	    // log.debug("text preeditCallback value={any}", .{preedit_});
2671	
2672	    // Crash metadata in case we crash in here
2673	    crash.sentry.thread_state = self.crashThreadState();
2674	    defer crash.sentry.thread_state = null;
2675	
2676	    self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
2677	    defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
2678	
2679	    // We clear our selection when ANY OF:
2680	    // 1. We have an existing preedit
2681	    // 2. We have preedit text
2682	    if (self.renderer_state.preedit != null or
2683	        preedit_ != null)
2684	    {
2685	        if (self.config.selection_clear_on_typing) {
2686	            self.setSelection(null) catch {};
2687	        }
2688	    }
2689	
2690	    // We always clear our prior preedit
2691	    if (self.renderer_state.preedit) |p| {
2692	        self.alloc.free(p.codepoints);
2693	        self.renderer_state.preedit = null;
2694	    }
2695	
2696	    // Mark preedit dirty flag
2697	    self.io.terminal.flags.dirty.preedit = true;
2698	
2699	    // If we have no text, we're done. We queue a render in case we cleared
2700	    // a prior preedit (likely).
2701	    const text = preedit_ orelse {
2702	        try self.queueRender();
2703	        return;
2704	    };
2705	
2706	    // We convert the UTF-8 text to codepoints.
2707	    const view = try std.unicode.Utf8View.init(text);
2708	    var it = view.iterator();
2709	
2710	    // Allocate the codepoints slice
2711	    const Codepoint = rendererpkg.State.Preedit.Codepoint;
2712	    var codepoints: std.ArrayList(Codepoint) = .empty;
2713	    defer codepoints.deinit(self.alloc);
2714	    while (it.nextCodepoint()) |cp| {
2715	        const width: usize = @intCast(unicode.table.get(cp).width);
2716	
2717	        // I've never seen a preedit text with a zero-width character. In
2718	        // theory its possible but we can't really handle it right now.
2719	        // Let's just ignore it.
2720	        if (width <= 0) continue;
2721	
2722	        try codepoints.append(
2723	            self.alloc,
2724	            .{ .codepoint = cp, .wide = width >= 2 },
2725	        );
2726	    }
2727	
2728	    // If we have no codepoints, then we're done.
2729	    if (codepoints.items.len == 0) {
2730	        try self.queueRender();
2731	        return;
2732	    }
2733	
2734	    self.renderer_state.preedit = .{
2735	        .codepoints = try codepoints.toOwnedSlice(self.alloc),
2736	    };
2737	    try self.queueRender();
2738	}
2792	pub fn keyCallback(
2793	    self: *Surface,
2794	    event_orig: input.KeyEvent,
2795	) !InputEffect {
2796	    // log.warn("text keyCallback event={}", .{event_orig});
2797	
2798	    // Apply key remappings to transform modifiers before any processing.
2799	    // This allows users to remap modifier keys at the app level.
2800	    var event = event_orig;
2801	    if (self.config.key_remaps.isRemapped(event_orig.mods)) {
2802	        event.mods = self.config.key_remaps.apply(event_orig.mods);
2803	    }
2804	
2805	    // Crash metadata in case we crash in here
2806	    crash.sentry.thread_state = self.crashThreadState();
2807	    defer crash.sentry.thread_state = null;
2808	
2809	    // Setup our inspector event if we have an inspector.
2810	    var insp_ev: ?inspectorpkg.KeyEvent = if (self.inspector != null) ev: {
2811	        var copy = event;
2812	        copy.utf8 = "";
2813	        if (event.utf8.len > 0) copy.utf8 = try self.alloc.dupe(u8, event.utf8);
2814	        break :ev .{ .event = copy };
2815	    } else null;
2816	
2817	    // When we're done processing, we always want to add the event to
2818	    // the inspector.
2819	    defer if (insp_ev) |ev| ev: {
2820	        // We have to check for the inspector again because our keybinding
2821	        // might close it.
2822	        const insp = self.inspector orelse {
2823	            ev.deinit(self.alloc);
2824	            break :ev;
2825	        };
2826	
2827	        if (insp.recordKeyEvent(self.alloc, ev)) {
2828	            self.queueRender() catch {};
2829	        } else |err| {
2830	            log.warn("error adding key event to inspector err={}", .{err});
2831	        }
2832	    };
2833	
2834	    // Handle keybindings first. We need to handle this on all events
2835	    // (press, repeat, release) because a press may perform a binding but
2836	    // a release should not encode if we consumed the press.
2837	    if (try self.maybeHandleBinding(
2838	        event,
2839	        if (insp_ev) |*ev| ev else null,
2840	    )) |v| return v;
2841	    // If we allow KAM and KAM is enabled then we do nothing.
2842	    if (self.config.vt_kam_allowed) {
2843	        self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
2844	        defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
2845	        if (self.io.terminal.modes.get(.disable_keyboard)) return .consumed;
2846	    }
2847	
2848	    // If this input event has text, then we hide the mouse if configured.
2849	    // We only do this on pressed events to avoid hiding the mouse when we
2850	    // change focus due to a keybinding (i.e. switching tabs).
2851	    if (self.config.mouse_hide_while_typing and
2852	        event.action == .press and
2853	        !self.mouse.hidden and
2854	        event.utf8.len > 0)
2855	    {
2856	        self.hideMouse();
2857	    }
2858	
2859	    // If our mouse modifiers change we may need to change our
2860	    // link highlight state.
2861	    if (!self.mouse.mods.equal(event.mods)) mouse_mods: {
2862	        // Update our modifiers, this will update mouse mods too
2863	        self.modsChanged(event.mods);
2864	
2865	        // We only refresh links if
2866	        // 1. mouse reporting is off
2867	        // OR
2868	        // 2. mouse reporting is on and we are not reporting shift to the terminal
2869	        if (self.io.terminal.flags.mouse_event == .none or
2870	            (self.mouse.mods.shift and !self.mouseShiftCapture(false)))
2871	        {
2872	            // Refresh our link state
2873	            const pos = self.rt_surface.getCursorPos() catch break :mouse_mods;
2874	            self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
2875	            defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
2876	            self.mouseRefreshLinks(
2877	                pos,
2878	                self.posToViewport(pos.x, pos.y),
2879	                self.mouse.over_link,
2880	            ) catch |err| {
2881	                log.warn("failed to refresh links err={}", .{err});
2882	                break :mouse_mods;
2883	            };
2884	        } else if (self.io.terminal.flags.mouse_event != .none and !self.mouse.mods.shift) {
2885	            // If we have mouse reports on and we don't have shift pressed, we reset state
2886	            _ = try self.rt_app.performAction(
2887	                .{ .surface = self },
2888	                .mouse_shape,
2889	                self.io.terminal.mouse_shape,
2890	            );
2891	            _ = try self.rt_app.performAction(
2892	                .{ .surface = self },
2893	                .mouse_over_link,
2894	                .{ .url = "" },
2895	            );
2896	            try self.queueRender();
2897	        }
2898	    }
2899	
2900	    // Process the cursor state logic. This will update the cursor shape if
2901	    // needed, depending on the key state.
2902	    if ((SurfaceMouse{
2903	        .physical_key = event.key,
2904	        .mouse_event = self.io.terminal.flags.mouse_event,
2905	        .mouse_shape = self.io.terminal.mouse_shape,
2906	        .mods = self.mouse.mods,
2907	        .over_link = self.mouse.over_link,
2908	        .hidden = self.mouse.hidden,
2909	    }).keyToMouseShape()) |shape| _ = try self.rt_app.performAction(
2910	        .{ .surface = self },
2911	        .mouse_shape,
2912	        shape,
2913	    );
2914	
2915	    // We've processed a key event that produced some data so we want to
2916	    // track the last pressed key.
2917	    self.pressed_key = event: {
2918	        // We need to unset the allocated fields that will become invalid
2919	        var copy = event;
2920	        copy.utf8 = "";
2921	
2922	        // If we have a previous pressed key and we're releasing it
2923	        // then we set it to invalid to prevent repeating the release event.
2924	        if (event.action == .release) {
2925	            // if we didn't have a previous event and this is a release
2926	            // event then we just want to set it to null.
2927	            const prev = self.pressed_key orelse break :event null;
2928	            if (prev.key == copy.key) copy.key = .unidentified;
2929	        }
2930	
2931	        // If our key is invalid and we have no mods, then we're done!
2932	        // This helps catch the state that we naturally released all keys.
2933	        if (copy.key == .unidentified and copy.mods.empty()) break :event null;
2934	
2935	        break :event copy;
2936	    };
2937	
2938	    // Encode and send our key. If we didn't encode anything, then we
2939	    // return the effect as ignored.
2940	    if (try self.encodeKey(
2941	        event,
2942	        if (insp_ev) |*ev| ev else null,
2943	    )) |write_req| {
2944	        // If our process is exited and we press a key that results in
