2945	        // an encoded value, we close the surface. We want to eventually
2946	        // move this behavior to the apprt probably.
2947	        if (self.child_exited) {
2948	            write_req.deinit();
2949	            self.close();
2950	            return .closed;
2951	        }
2952	
2953	        self.queueIo(switch (write_req) {
2954	            .small => |v| .{ .write_small = v },
2955	            .stable => |v| .{ .write_stable = v },
2956	            .alloc => |v| .{ .write_alloc = v },
2957	        }, .unlocked);
2958	    } else {
2959	        // No valid request means that we didn't encode anything.
2960	        return .ignored;
2961	    }
2962	
2963	    // If our event is any keypress that isn't a modifier and we generated
2964	    // some data to send to the pty, then we move the viewport down to the
2965	    // bottom. We also clear the selection for any key other then modifiers.
2966	    if (!event.key.modifier()) {
2967	        self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
2968	        defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
2969	
2970	        if (self.config.selection_clear_on_typing or
2971	            event.key == .escape)
2972	        {
2973	            try self.setSelection(null);
2974	        }
2975	
2976	        if (self.config.scroll_to_bottom.keystroke) self.io.terminal.scrollViewport(.bottom);
2977	
2978	        try self.queueRender();
2979	    }
2980	
2981	    return .consumed;
2982	}
2984	/// Maybe handles a binding for a given event and if so returns the effect.
2985	/// Returns null if the event is not handled in any way and processing should
2986	/// continue.
2987	fn maybeHandleBinding(
2988	    self: *Surface,
2989	    event: input.KeyEvent,
2990	    insp_ev: ?*inspectorpkg.KeyEvent,
2991	) !?InputEffect {
2992	    switch (event.action) {
2993	        // Release events never trigger a binding but we need to check if
2994	        // we consumed the press event so we don't encode the release.
2995	        .release => {
2996	            if (self.keyboard.last_trigger) |last| {
2997	                if (last == event.bindingHash()) {
2998	                    // We don't reset the last trigger on release because
2999	                    // an apprt may send multiple release events for a single
3000	                    // press event.
3001	                    return .consumed;
3002	                }
3003	            }
3004	
3005	            return null;
3006	        },
3007	
3008	        // Carry on processing.
3009	        .press, .repeat => {},
3010	    }
3011	
3012	    // Find an entry in the keybind set that matches our event.
3013	    const entry: input.Binding.Set.Entry = entry: {
3014	        // Handle key sequences first.
3015	        if (self.keyboard.sequence_set) |set| {
3016	            // Get our entry from the set for the given event.
3017	            if (set.getEvent(event)) |v| break :entry v;
3018	
3019	            // No entry found. We need to encode everything up to this
3020	            // point and send to the pty since we're in a sequence.
3021	
3022	            // We ignore modifiers so that nested sequences such as
3023	            // ctrl+a>ctrl+b>c work.
3024	            if (event.key.modifier()) return null;
3025	
3026	            // If we have a catch-all of ignore, then we special case our
3027	            // invalid sequence handling to ignore it.
3028	            if (self.catchAllIsIgnore()) {
3029	                self.endKeySequence(.drop, .retain);
3030	                return .ignored;
3031	            }
3032	
3033	            // Encode everything up to this point
3034	            self.endKeySequence(.flush, .retain);
3035	
3036	            return null;
3037	        }
3038	
3039	        // No currently active sequence, move on to tables. For tables,
3040	        // we search inner-most table to outer-most. The table stack does
3041	        // NOT include the root set.
3042	        const table_items = self.keyboard.table_stack.items;
3043	        if (table_items.len > 0) {
3044	            for (0..table_items.len) |i| {
3045	                const rev_i: usize = table_items.len - 1 - i;
3046	                const table = table_items[rev_i];
3047	                if (table.set.getEvent(event)) |v| {
3048	                    // If this is a one-shot activation AND its the currently
3049	                    // active table, then we deactivate it after this.
3050	                    // Note: we may want to change the semantics here to
3051	                    // remove this table no matter where it is in the stack,
3052	                    // maybe.
3053	                    if (table.once and i == 0) _ = try self.performBindingAction(
3054	                        .deactivate_key_table,
3055	                    );
3056	
3057	                    break :entry v;
3058	                }
3059	            }
3060	        }
3061	
3062	        // No table, use our default set
3063	        break :entry self.config.keybind.set.getEvent(event) orelse
3064	            return null;
3065	    };
3066	
3067	    // Determine if this entry has an action or if its a leader key.
3068	    const leaf: input.Binding.Set.GenericLeaf = switch (entry.value_ptr.*) {
3069	        .leader => |set| {
3070	            // Store this event so that we can drain and encode on invalid.
3071	            // We don't need to cap this because it is naturally capped by
3072	            // the config validation.
3073	            if (try self.encodeKey(event, insp_ev)) |req| {
3074	                self.keyboard.sequence_queued.append(self.alloc, req) catch |err| {
3075	                    req.deinit();
3076	                    return err;
3077	                };
3078	            }
3079	
3080	            // Setup the next set we'll look at only after all fallible work.
3081	            self.keyboard.sequence_set = set;
3082	
3083	            // Start or continue our key sequence
3084	            _ = self.rt_app.performAction(
3085	                .{ .surface = self },
3086	                .key_sequence,
3087	                .{ .trigger = entry.key_ptr.* },
3088	            ) catch |err| {
3089	                log.warn(
3090	                    "failed to notify app of key sequence err={}",
3091	                    .{err},
3092	                );
3093	            };
3094	
3095	            return .consumed;
3096	        },
3097	
3098	        inline .leaf, .leaf_chained => |leaf| leaf.generic(),
3099	    };
3100	
3101	    // consumed determines if the input is consumed or if we continue
3102	    // encoding the key (if we have a key to encode).
3103	    const consumed = consumed: {
3104	        // If the consumed flag is explicitly set, then we are consumed.
3105	        if (leaf.flags.consumed) break :consumed true;
3106	
3107	        // If the global or all flag is set, we always consume.
3108	        if (leaf.flags.global or leaf.flags.all) break :consumed true;
3109	
3110	        break :consumed false;
3111	    };
3112	
3113	    // We have an action, so at this point we're handling SOMETHING so
3114	    // we reset the last trigger to null. We only set this if we actually
3115	    // perform an action (below)
3116	    self.keyboard.last_trigger = null;
3117	
3118	    // An action also always resets the sequence set.
3119	    self.keyboard.sequence_set = null;
3120	
3121	    // Setup our actions
3122	    const actions = leaf.actionsSlice();
3123	
3124	    // Attempt to perform the action
3125	    log.debug("key event binding flags={} action={any}", .{
3126	        leaf.flags,
3127	        actions,
3128	    });
3129	    const performed = performed: {
3130	        // If this is a global or all action, then we perform it on
3131	        // the app and it applies to every surface.
3132	        if (leaf.flags.global or leaf.flags.all) {
3133	            self.app.performAllChainedAction(
3134	                self.rt_app,
3135	                actions,
3136	            );
3137	
3138	            // "All" actions are always performed since they are global.
3139	            break :performed true;
3140	        }
3141	
3142	        // Perform each action. We are performed if ANY of the chained
3143	        // actions perform.
3144	        var performed: bool = false;
3145	        for (actions) |action| {
3146	            if (self.performBindingAction(action)) |v| {
3147	                performed = performed or v;
3148	            } else |err| {
3149	                log.info(
3150	                    "key binding action failed action={t} err={}",
3151	                    .{ action, err },
3152	                );
3153	            }
3154	        }
3155	
3156	        break :performed performed;
3157	    };
3158	
3159	    if (performed) {
3160	        // If we performed an action and it was a closing action,
3161	        // our "self" pointer is not safe to use anymore so we need to
3162	        // just exit immediately.
3163	        for (actions) |action| if (closingAction(action)) {
3164	            log.debug("key binding is a closing binding, halting key event processing", .{});
3165	            return .closed;
3166	        };
3167	
3168	        // If our action was "ignore" then we return the special input
3169	        // effect of "ignored".
3170	        for (actions) |action| if (action == .ignore) {
3171	            // If we're in a sequence, clear it.
3172	            self.endKeySequence(.drop, .retain);
3173	
3174	            return .ignored;
3175	        };
3176	    }
3177	
3178	    // If we have the performable flag and the action was not performed,
3179	    // then we act as though a binding didn't exist.
3180	    if (leaf.flags.performable and !performed) {
3181	        // If we're in a sequence, we treat this as if we pressed a key
3182	        // that doesn't exist in the sequence. Reset our sequence and flush
3183	        // any queued events.
3184	        self.endKeySequence(.flush, .retain);
3185	
3186	        return null;
3187	    }
3188	
3189	    // If we consume this event, then we are done. If we don't consume
3190	    // it, we processed the action but we still want to process our
3191	    // encodings, too.
3192	    if (consumed) {
3193	        // If we had queued events, we deinit them since we consumed
3194	        self.endKeySequence(.drop, .retain);
3195	
3196	        // Store our last trigger so we don't encode the release event
3197	        self.keyboard.last_trigger = event.bindingHash();
3198	
3199	        if (insp_ev) |ev| {
3200	            ev.binding = self.alloc.dupe(
3201	                input.Binding.Action,
3202	                actions,
3203	            ) catch |err| binding: {
3204	                log.warn(
3205	                    "error allocating binding action for inspector err={}",
3206	                    .{err},
3207	                );
3208	                break :binding &.{};
3209	            };
3210	        }
3211	        return .consumed;
3212	    }
3213	
3214	    // If we didn't perform OR we didn't consume, then we want to
3215	    // encode any queued events for a sequence.
3216	    self.endKeySequence(.flush, .retain);
3217	
3218	    return null;
3219	}
3280	fn endKeySequence(
3281	    self: *Surface,
3282	    action: KeySequenceQueued,
3283	    mem: KeySequenceMemory,
3284	) void {
3285	    // Notify apprt key sequence ended
3286	    _ = self.rt_app.performAction(
3287	        .{ .surface = self },
3288	        .key_sequence,
3289	        .end,
3290	    ) catch |err| {
3291	        log.warn(
3292	            "failed to notify app of key sequence end err={}",
3293	            .{err},
3294	        );
3295	    };
3296	
3297	    // No matter what we clear our current sequence set. This restores
3298	    // the set we look at to the root set.
3299	    self.keyboard.sequence_set = null;
3300	
3301	    // If we have no queued data, there is nothing else to do.
3302	    if (self.keyboard.sequence_queued.items.len == 0) return;
3303	
3304	    // Run the proper action first
3305	    switch (action) {
3306	        .flush => for (self.keyboard.sequence_queued.items) |write_req| {
3307	            self.queueIo(switch (write_req) {
3308	                .small => |v| .{ .write_small = v },
3309	                .stable => |v| .{ .write_stable = v },
3310	                .alloc => |v| .{ .write_alloc = v },
3311	            }, .unlocked);
3312	        },
3313	
3314	        .drop => for (self.keyboard.sequence_queued.items) |req| req.deinit(),
3315	    }
3316	
3317	    // Memory handling of the sequence after the action
3318	    switch (mem) {
3319	        .free => self.keyboard.sequence_queued.clearAndFree(self.alloc),
3320	        .retain => self.keyboard.sequence_queued.clearRetainingCapacity(),
3321	    }
3322	}
3324	/// Encodes the key event into a write request. The write request will
3325	/// always copy or allocate so the caller can safely free the event.
3326	fn encodeKey(
3327	    self: *Surface,
3328	    event: input.KeyEvent,
3329	    insp_ev: ?*inspectorpkg.KeyEvent,
3330	) !?termio.Message.WriteReq {
3331	    const write_req: termio.Message.WriteReq = req: {
3332	        // Build our encoding options, which requires the lock.
3333	        const encoding_opts = self.encodeKeyOpts();
3334	
3335	        // Try to write the input into a small array. This fits almost
3336	        // every scenario. Larger situations can happen due to long
3337	        // pre-edits.
3338	        var data: termio.Message.WriteReq.Small.Array = undefined;
3339	        var writer: std.Io.Writer = .fixed(&data);
3340	        if (input.key_encode.encode(
3341	            &writer,
3342	            event,
3343	            encoding_opts,
3344	        )) {
3345	            const written = writer.buffered();
3346	
3347	            // Special-case: we did nothing.
3348	            if (written.len == 0) return null;
3349	
3350	            break :req .{ .small = .{
3351	                .data = data,
3352	                .len = @intCast(written.len),
3353	            } };
3354	        } else |err| switch (err) {
3355	            // Means we need to allocate
3356	            error.WriteFailed => {},
3357	        }
3358	
3359	        // We need to allocate. We allocate double the UTF-8 length
3360	        // or double the small array size, whichever is larger. That's
3361	        // a heuristic that should work. The only scenario I know while
3362	        // typing this where we don't have enough space is a long preedit,
3363	        // and in that case the size we need is exactly the UTF-8 length,
3364	        // so the double is being safe.
3365	        var alloc_writer: std.Io.Writer.Allocating = try .initCapacity(
3366	            self.alloc,
3367	            @max(event.utf8.len * 2, data.len * 2),
3368	        );
3369	        defer alloc_writer.deinit();
3370	
3371	        try input.key_encode.encode(
3372	            &alloc_writer.writer,
3373	            event,
3374	            encoding_opts,
3375	        );
3376	        break :req .{ .alloc = .{
3377	            .alloc = self.alloc,
3378	            .data = try alloc_writer.toOwnedSlice(),
3379	        } };
3380	    };
3381	
3382	    // Copy the encoded data into the inspector event if we have one.
3383	    // We do this before the mailbox because the IO thread could
3384	    // release the memory before we get a chance to copy it.
3385	    if (insp_ev) |ev| pty: {
3386	        const slice = write_req.slice();
3387	        const copy = self.alloc.alloc(u8, slice.len) catch |err| {
3388	            log.warn("error allocating pty data for inspector err={}", .{err});
3389	            break :pty;
3390	        };
3391	        errdefer self.alloc.free(copy);
3392	        @memcpy(copy, slice);
3393	        ev.pty = copy;
3394	    }
3395	
3396	    return write_req;
3397	}
3398	
3399	fn encodeKeyOpts(self: *const Surface) input.key_encode.Options {
3400	    self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
3401	    defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
3402	    const t = &self.io.terminal;
3403	
3404	    var opts: input.key_encode.Options = .fromTerminal(t);
3405	    if (comptime builtin.target.os.tag != .macos) return opts;
3406	
3407	    opts.macos_option_as_alt = self.config.macos_option_as_alt orelse detect: {
3408	        // If we don't have alt pressed, it doesn't matter what this
3409	        // config is so we can just say "false" and break out and avoid
3410	        // more expensive checks below.
3411	        if (!self.mouse.mods.alt) break :detect .false;
3412	
3413	        // Alt is pressed, we're on macOS. We break some encapsulation
3414	        // here and assume libghostty for ease...
3415	        break :detect self.rt_app.keyboardLayout().detectOptionAsAlt();
3416	    };
3417	
3418	    return opts;
3419	}
3420	
3421	/// Sends text as-is to the terminal without triggering any keyboard
3422	/// protocol. This will treat the input text as if it was pasted
3423	/// from the clipboard so the same logic will be applied. Namely,
3424	/// if bracketed mode is on this will do a bracketed paste. Otherwise,
3425	/// this will filter newlines to '\r'.
3426	pub fn textCallback(self: *Surface, text: []const u8) !void {
3427	    // Crash metadata in case we crash in here
3428	    crash.sentry.thread_state = self.crashThreadState();
3429	    defer crash.sentry.thread_state = null;
3430	
3431	    try self.completeClipboardPaste(text, true);
3432	}
3437	pub fn occlusionCallback(self: *Surface, visible: bool) !void {
3438	    // Crash metadata in case we crash in here
3439	    crash.sentry.thread_state = self.crashThreadState();
3440	    defer crash.sentry.thread_state = null;
3441	
3442	    // Avoid duplicate renderer and visibility reports.
3443	    if (self.visible == visible) return;
3444	    self.visible = visible;
3445	
3446	    // Update the terminal state for synchronous queries, then notify the IO
3447	    // thread so it can emit a mode 2033 report when enabled.
3448	    self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
3449	    self.io.terminal.flags.visible = visible;
3450	    const report_visibility = self.io.terminal.modes.get(.report_visibility);
3451	    self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
3452	    if (report_visibility) {
3453	        self.queueIo(.{ .visibility_report = .{
3454	            .visible = visible,
3455	            .force = false,
3456	        } }, .unlocked);
3457	    }
3458	
3459	    _ = self.renderer_thread.mailbox.push(global.io(), .{
3460	        .visible = visible,
3461	    }, .{ .forever = {} });
3462	
3463	    try self.queueRender();
3464	}
3465	
3466	pub fn focusCallback(self: *Surface, focused: bool) !void {
3467	    // Crash metadata in case we crash in here
3468	    crash.sentry.thread_state = self.crashThreadState();
3469	    defer crash.sentry.thread_state = null;
3470	
3471	    // Always update the app focused surface, otherwise we miss
3472	    // the first surface created.
3473	    if (focused) self.app.focusSurface(self);
3474	
3475	    // If our focus state is unchanged we do nothing else.
3476	    if (self.focused == focused) return;
3477	    self.focused = focused;
3478	
3479	    // Notify our render thread of the new state
3480	    _ = self.renderer_thread.mailbox.push(global.io(), .{
3481	        .focus = focused,
3482	    }, .{ .forever = {} });
3483	
3484	    if (!focused) unfocused: {
3485	        // If we lost focus and we have a keypress, then we want to send a key
3486	        // release event for it. Depending on the apprt, this CAN result in
3487	        // duplicate key release events, but that is better than not sending
3488	        // a key release event at all.
3489	        var pressed_key = self.pressed_key orelse break :unfocused;
3490	        self.pressed_key = null;
3491	
3492	        // All our actions will be releases
3493	        pressed_key.action = .release;
3494	
3495	        // Release the full key first
3496	        if (pressed_key.key != .unidentified) {
3497	            assert(self.keyCallback(pressed_key) catch |err| err: {
3498	                log.warn("error releasing key on focus loss err={}", .{err});
3499	                break :err .ignored;
3500	            } != .closed);
3501	        }
3502	
3503	        // Release any modifiers if set
3504	        if (pressed_key.mods.empty()) break :unfocused;
3505	
3506	        // This is kind of nasty comptime meta programming but all we're doing
3507	        // here is going through all the modifiers and if they're set, releasing
3508	        // both the left and right sides of the modifier. This may not match
3509	        // the exact input event but it ensures a full reset.
3510	        const keys = &.{ "shift", "ctrl", "alt", "super" };
3511	        const original_key = pressed_key.key;
3512	        inline for (keys) |key| {
3513	            if (@field(pressed_key.mods, key)) {
3514	                @field(pressed_key.mods, key) = false;
3515	                inline for (&.{ "right", "left" }) |side| {
3516	                    const keyname = comptime keyname: {
3517	                        break :keyname if (std.mem.eql(u8, key, "ctrl"))
3518	                            "control"
3519	                        else if (std.mem.eql(u8, key, "super"))
3520	                            "meta"
3521	                        else
3522	                            key;
3523	                    };
3524	                    pressed_key.key = @field(input.Key, keyname ++ "_" ++ side);
3525	                    if (pressed_key.key != original_key) {
3526	                        assert(self.keyCallback(pressed_key) catch |err| err: {
3527	                            log.warn("error releasing key on focus loss err={}", .{err});
3528	                            break :err .ignored;
3529	                        } != .closed);
3530	                    }
3531	                }
3532	            }
3533	        }
3534	    }
3535	
3536	    // Schedule render which also drains our mailbox
3537	    try self.queueRender();
3538	
3539	    // Whenever our focus changes we unhide the mouse. The mouse will be
3540	    // hidden again if the user starts typing. This helps alleviate some
3541	    // buggy behavior upstream in macOS with the mouse never becoming visible
3542	    // again when tabbing between programs (see #2525).
3543	    self.showMouse();
3544	
3545	    // Update the focus state and notify the terminal
3546	    {
3547	        self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
3548	        self.io.terminal.flags.focused = focused;
3549	        self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
3550	        self.queueIo(.{ .focused = focused }, .unlocked);
3551	    }
3552	}
3763	/// This is called when the content scale of the surface changes. The surface
3764	/// can then update any DPI-sensitive state.
3765	pub fn contentScaleCallback(self: *Surface, content_scale: apprt.ContentScale) !void {
3766	    // Crash metadata in case we crash in here
3767	    crash.sentry.thread_state = self.crashThreadState();
3768	    defer crash.sentry.thread_state = null;
3769	
3770	    // Calculate the new DPI
3771	    const x_dpi = content_scale.x * font.face.default_dpi;
3772	    const y_dpi = content_scale.y * font.face.default_dpi;
3773	
3774	    // Update our font size which is dependent on the DPI
3775	    const size = size: {
3776	        var size = self.font_size;
3777	        size.xdpi = @intFromFloat(@round(x_dpi));
3778	        size.ydpi = @intFromFloat(@round(y_dpi));
3779	        break :size size;
3780	    };
3781	
3782	    // If our DPI didn't actually change, save a lot of work by doing nothing.
3783	    if (size.xdpi == self.font_size.xdpi and size.ydpi == self.font_size.ydpi) {
3784	        return;
3785	    }
3786	
3787	    try self.setFontSize(size);
3788	
3789	    // Update our padding which is dependent on DPI. We only do this for
3790	    // unbalanced padding since balanced padding is not dependent on DPI.
3791	    if (self.config.window_padding_balance == .false) {
3792	        self.size.padding = self.config.scaledPadding(x_dpi, y_dpi);
3793	    }
3794	
3795	    // Force a resize event because the change in padding will affect
3796	    // pixel-level changes to the renderer and viewport.
3797	    try self.resize(self.size.screen);
3798	}
3802	fn isMouseReporting(self: *const Surface) bool {
3803	    return self.config.mouse_reporting and
3804	        self.io.terminal.flags.mouse_event != .none;
3805	}
3813	fn mouseReport(
3814	    self: *Surface,
3815	    button: ?input.MouseButton,
3816	    action: input.MouseAction,
3817	    mods: input.Mods,
3818	    pos: apprt.CursorPos,
3819	) void {
3820	    // Mouse reporting must be enabled by both config and terminal state
3821	    assert(self.config.mouse_reporting);
3822	    assert(self.io.terminal.flags.mouse_event != .none);
3823	
3824	    // Build our encoding options.
3825	    const encoding_opts: input.mouse_encode.Options = opts: {
3826	        // Terminal and size state.
3827	        var opts: input.mouse_encode.Options = .fromTerminal(
3828	            &self.io.terminal,
3829	            self.size,
3830	        );
3831	
3832	        // Whether any button is pressed at all.
3833	        opts.any_button_pressed = pressed: {
3834	            for (self.mouse.click_state) |state| {
3835	                if (state != .release) break :pressed true;
3836	            }
3837	
3838	            break :pressed false;
3839	        };
3840	
3841	        // Keep track of our last reported viewport cell for event
3842	        // deduplication.
3843	        opts.last_cell = &self.mouse.event_point;
3844	
3845	        break :opts opts;
3846	    };
3847	
3848	    var data: termio.Message.WriteReq.Small.Array = undefined;
3849	    var writer: std.Io.Writer = .fixed(&data);
3850	    input.mouse_encode.encode(&writer, .{
3851	        .button = button,
3852	        .action = action,
3853	        .mods = mods,
3854	        .pos = .{
3855	            .x = pos.x,
3856	            .y = pos.y,
3857	        },
3858	    }, encoding_opts) catch |err| switch (err) {
3859	        error.WriteFailed => {
3860	            // This should never happen since mouse events should never
3861	            // be able to overflow the size of our small array. But if it
3862	            // does, let's log it and return. No need to crash upstreams.
3863	            // In the future we may want to fall back to allocation.
3864	            log.warn("failed to encode mouse event err={}", .{err});
3865	            return;
3866	        },
3867	    };
3868	    const written = writer.buffered();
3869	    if (written.len == 0) return;
3870	
3871	    self.queueIo(.{ .write_small = .{
3872	        .data = data,
3873	        .len = @intCast(written.len),
3874	    } }, .locked);
3875	}
3880	fn mouseShiftCapture(self: *const Surface, lock: bool) bool {
3881	    // Handle our never/always case where we don't need a lock.
3882	    switch (self.config.mouse_shift_capture) {
3883	        .never => return false,
3884	        .always => return true,
3885	        .false, .true => {},
3886	    }
3887	
3888	    if (lock) self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
3889	    defer if (lock) self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
3890	
3891	    // If the terminal explicitly requests it then we always allow it
3892	    // since we processed never/always at this point.
3893	    switch (self.io.terminal.flags.mouse_shift_capture) {
3894	        .false => return false,
3895	        .true => return true,
3896	        .null => {},
3897	    }
3898	
3899	    // Otherwise, go with the user's preference
3900	    return switch (self.config.mouse_shift_capture) {
3901	        .false => false,
3902	        .true => true,
3903	        .never, .always => unreachable, // handled earlier
3904	    };
3905	}
3918	pub fn mouseButtonCallback(
3919	    self: *Surface,
3920	    action: input.MouseButtonState,
3921	    button: input.MouseButton,
3922	    mods: input.Mods,
3923	) !bool {
3924	    // Crash metadata in case we crash in here
3925	    crash.sentry.thread_state = self.crashThreadState();
3926	    defer crash.sentry.thread_state = null;
3927	
3928	    // log.debug("mouse action={} button={} mods={}", .{ action, button, mods });
3929	
3930	    // If we have an inspector, we always queue a render
3931	    if (self.inspector != null) {
3932	        defer self.queueRender() catch {};
3933	    }
3934	
3935	    // Always record our latest mouse state
3936	    self.mouse.click_state[@intCast(@backingInt(button))] = action;
3937	
3938	    // Always show the mouse again if it is hidden
3939	    if (self.mouse.hidden) self.showMouse();
3940	
3941	    // Update our modifiers if they changed
3942	    self.modsChanged(mods);
3943	
3944	    // This is set to true if the terminal is allowed to capture the shift
3945	    // modifier. Note we can do this more efficiently probably with less
3946	    // locking/unlocking but clicking isn't that frequent enough to be a
3947	    // bottleneck.
3948	    const shift_capture = self.mouseShiftCapture(true);
3949	
3950	    // Shift-click continues the previous mouse state if we have a selection.
3951	    // cursorPosCallback will also do a mouse report so we don't need to do any
3952	    // of the logic below.
3953	    if (button == .left and action == .press) {
3954	        // We could do all the conditionals in one but I find it more
3955	        // readable as a human to break this one up.
3956	        if (mods.shift and
3957	            self.mouse.selection_gesture.left_click_count > 0 and
3958	            !shift_capture)
3959	        extend_selection: {
3960	            // We split this conditional out on its own because this is the
3961	            // only one that requires a renderer mutex grab which is VERY
3962	            // expensive because it could block all our threads.
3963	            if (!self.hasSelection()) break :extend_selection;
3964	
3965	            // If we are within the interval that the click would register
3966	            // an increment then we do not extend the selection.
3967	            const click_time = self.mouse.selection_gesture.left_click_time orelse
3968	                break :extend_selection;
3969	            const since = click_time.untilNow(global.io(), .awake);
3970	            if (since.toNanoseconds() <= self.config.mouse_interval) {
3971	                // Click interval very short, we may be increasing
3972	                // click counts so we don't extend the selection.
3973	                break :extend_selection;
3974	            }
3975	
3976	            const pos = try self.rt_surface.getCursorPos();
3977	            try self.cursorPosCallback(pos, null);
3978	            return true;
3979	        }
3980	    }
3981	
3982	    if (button == .left and action == .release) {
3983	        self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
3984	        defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
3985	
3986	        // The selection gesture tracks whether a press became a drag by
3987	        // comparing the release cell to the original press cell. Resolve the
3988	        // release position and pin before notifying the gesture so later
3989	        // release handling can query that state.
3990	        const release_pos: ?apprt.CursorPos = self.rt_surface.getCursorPos() catch |err| pos: {
3991	            log.warn("error reading cursor position for mouse release err={}", .{err});
3992	            break :pos null;
3993	        };
3994	
3995	        // If we can't map the release position to a cell, pass null so the
3996	        // gesture can conservatively treat the release as having moved away
3997	        // from the pressed cell.
3998	        const release_pin: ?terminal.Pin = if (release_pos) |pos| pin: {
3999	            const release_vp = self.posToViewport(pos.x, pos.y);
4000	            break :pin self.io.terminal.screens.active.pages.pin(.{ .viewport = .{
4001	                .x = release_vp.x,
4002	                .y = release_vp.y,
4003	            } });
4004	        } else null;
4005	        self.mouse.selection_gesture.release(
4006	            self.renderer_state.terminal,
4007	            .{ .pin = release_pin },
4008	        );
4009	
4010	        // Stop selection scrolling when releasing the left mouse button
4011	        // but only when selection scrolling is active.
4012	        if (self.selection_scroll_active) {
4013	            self.queueIo(
4014	                .{ .selection_scroll = false },
4015	                .locked,
4016	            );
4017	        }
4018	
4019	        // The selection clipboard is only updated for left-click drag when
4020	        // the left button is released. This is to avoid the clipboard
4021	        // being updated on every mouse move which would be noisy.
4022	        if (self.config.copy_on_select != .none) {
4023	            const prev_ = self.io.terminal.screens.active.selection;
4024	            if (prev_) |prev| {
4025	                try self.setSelectionAndCopy(terminal.Selection.init(
4026	                    prev.start(),
4027	                    prev.end(),
4028	                    prev.rectangle,
4029	                ));
4030	            }
4031	        }
4032	
4033	        // Handle link clicking. We want to do this before we do mouse
4034	        // reporting or any other mouse handling because a successfully
4035	        // clicked link will swallow the event.
4036	        if (self.mouse.over_link and !self.mouse.selection_gesture.left_click_dragged) {
4037	            // We are holding the renderer lock, but this should just be
4038	            // a cached value.
4039	            const pos = release_pos orelse try self.rt_surface.getCursorPos();
4040	            if (self.processLinks(pos)) |processed| {
4041	                if (processed) return true;
4042	            } else |err| {
4043	                log.warn("error processing links err={}", .{err});
4044	            }
4045	        }
4046	
4047	        // Handle prompt clicking. If we released our mouse on a prompt
4048	        // and we support some kind of click events, then we need to
4049	        // move to it.
4050	        if (self.maybePromptClick()) |handled| {
4051	            if (handled) return true;
4052	        } else |err| {
4053	            log.warn("error processing prompt click err={}", .{err});
4054	        }
4055	    }
4056	
4057	    // Report mouse events if enabled
4058	    {
4059	        self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
4060	        defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
4061	        if (self.isMouseReporting()) report: {
4062	            // If we have shift-pressed and we aren't allowed to capture it,
4063	            // then we do not do a mouse report.
4064	            if (mods.shift and !shift_capture) break :report;
4065	
4066	            // In any other mouse button scenario without shift pressed we
4067	            // clear the selection since the underlying application can handle
4068	            // that in any way (i.e. "scrolling").
4069	            try self.setSelection(null);
4070	
4071	            // We also set the left click count to 0 so that if mouse reporting
4072	            // is disabled in the middle of press (before release) we don't
4073	            // suddenly start selecting text.
4074	            self.mouse.selection_gesture.reset(self.renderer_state.terminal);
4075	
4076	            const pos = try self.rt_surface.getCursorPos();
4077	
4078	            const report_action: input.MouseAction = switch (action) {
4079	                .press => .press,
4080	                .release => .release,
4081	            };
4082	
4083	            self.mouseReport(
4084	                button,
4085	                report_action,
4086	                self.mouse.mods,
4087	                pos,
4088	            );
4089	
4090	            // If we're doing mouse reporting, we do not support any other
4091	            // selection or highlighting.
4092	            return true;
4093	        }
4094	    }
4095	
4096	    // For left button clicks we always record some information for
4097	    // selection/highlighting purposes.
4098	    if (button == .left and action == .press) click: {
4099	        self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
4100	        defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
4101	        const t: *terminal.Terminal = self.renderer_state.terminal;
4102	        const screen: *terminal.Screen = self.renderer_state.terminal.screens.active;
4103	
4104	        const pos = try self.rt_surface.getCursorPos();
4105	        const pin = pin: {
4106	            const pt_viewport = self.posToViewport(pos.x, pos.y);
4107	            const pin = screen.pages.pin(.{
4108	                .viewport = .{
4109	                    .x = pt_viewport.x,
4110	                    .y = pt_viewport.y,
4111	                },
4112	            }) orelse {
4113	                // Weird... our viewport x/y that we just converted isn't
4114	                // found in our pages. This is probably a bug but we don't
4115	                // want to crash in releases because its harmless. So, we
4116	                // only assert in debug mode.
4117	                if (comptime std.debug.runtime_safety) unreachable;
4118	                break :click;
4119	            };
4120	
4121	            break :pin pin;
4122	        };
4123	
4124	        var press_selection = try self.mouse.selection_gesture.press(t, .{
4125	            .time = std.Io.Timestamp.now(global.io(), .awake),
4126	            .pin = pin,
4127	            .xpos = pos.x,
4128	            .ypos = pos.y,
4129	            .max_distance = @floatFromInt(self.size.cell.width),
4130	            .repeat_interval = self.config.mouse_interval,
4131	            .word_boundary_codepoints = self.config.selection_word_chars,
4132	            .behaviors = &.{
4133	                .cell,
4134	                .word,
4135	                if (mods.ctrlOrSuper()) .output else .line,
4136	            },
4137	        });
4138	
4139	        // The gesture owns the standard single/double/triple-click selection
4140	        // behavior. Surface keeps terminal-surface-specific overrides here.
4141	        switch (self.mouse.selection_gesture.left_click_count) {
4142	            1 => {},
4143	
4144	            // Double click on a URL selects the entire URL instead of the
4145	            // standard word selection returned by the gesture.
4146	            2 => {
4147	                // Try link detection without requiring modifier keys.
4148	                if (self.linkAtPin(
4149	                    pin,
4150	                    null,
4151	                )) |result_| {
4152	                    if (result_) |result| {
4153	                        press_selection = result.selection;
4154	                    }
4155	                } else |_| {
4156	                    // Ignore any errors, likely regex errors.
4157	                }
4158	            },
4159	
4160	            3 => {},
4161	
4162	            // We should be bounded by 1 to 3
4163	            else => unreachable,
4164	        }
4165	
4166	        // Use `setSelection` (not `setSelectionAndCopy`) here to avoid
4167	        // touching the selection clipboard: for left mouse clicks we only
4168	        // copy on release.
4169	        if (press_selection) |selection| {
4170	            try self.setSelection(selection);
4171	            try self.queueRender();
4172	        } else if (self.mouse.selection_gesture.left_click_count == 1 and
4173	            self.io.terminal.screens.active.selection != null)
4174	        {
4175	            try self.setSelection(null);
4176	            try self.queueRender();
4177	        }
4178	    }
4179	
4180	    // Middle-click action, either ignore, or paste from clipboard or paste from the selection clipboard if supported.
4181	    if (button == .middle and action == .press) switch (self.config.middle_click_action) {
4182	        .ignore => {},
4183	        .@"clipboard-paste" => {
4184	            _ = try self.startClipboardRequest(.standard, .{ .paste = .standard });
4185	        },
4186	        .@"primary-paste" => {
4187	            if (self.rt_surface.supportsClipboard(.selection)) {
4188	                _ = try self.startClipboardRequest(.selection, .{ .paste = .selection });
4189	            }
4190	        },
4191	    };
4192	
4193	    // Right-click down selects word for context menus. If the apprt
4194	    // doesn't implement context menus this can be a bit weird but they
4195	    // are supported by our two main apprts so we always do this. If we
4196	    // want to be careful in the future we can add a function to apprts
4197	    // that let's us know.
4198	    if (button == .right and action == .press) sel: {
4199	        self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
4200	        defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
4201	
4202	        // Get our viewport pin
4203	        const screen: *terminal.Screen = self.renderer_state.terminal.screens.active;
4204	        const pos = try self.rt_surface.getCursorPos();
4205	        const pin = pin: {
4206	            const pt_viewport = self.posToViewport(pos.x, pos.y);
4207	            const pin = screen.pages.pin(.{
4208	                .viewport = .{
4209	                    .x = pt_viewport.x,
4210	                    .y = pt_viewport.y,
4211	                },
4212	            }) orelse {
4213	                if (comptime std.debug.runtime_safety) unreachable;
4214	                break :sel;
4215	            };
4216	
4217	            break :pin pin;
4218	        };
4219	
4220	        switch (self.config.right_click_action) {
4221	            .ignore => {},
4222	            .@"context-menu" => {
4223	                // If we already have a selection and the selection contains
4224	                // where we clicked then we don't want to modify the selection.
4225	                if (self.io.terminal.screens.active.selection) |prev_sel| {
4226	                    if (prev_sel.contains(screen, pin)) break :sel;
4227	
4228	                    // The selection doesn't contain our pin, so we create a new
4229	                    // word selection where we clicked.
4230	                }
4231	
4232	                // If there is a link at this position, we want to
4233	                // select the link. Otherwise, select the word.
4234	                if (try self.linkAtPos(pos)) |link| {
4235	                    try self.setSelectionAndCopy(link.selection);
4236	                } else {
4237	                    const sel = screen.selectWord(
4238	                        pin,
4239	                        self.config.selection_word_chars,
4240	                    ) orelse break :sel;
4241	                    try self.setSelectionAndCopy(sel);
4242	                }
4243	                try self.queueRender();
4244	
4245	                // Don't consume so that we show the context menu in apprt.
4246	                return false;
4247	            },
4248	            .copy => {
4249	                if (self.io.terminal.screens.active.selection) |sel| {
4250	                    try self.copySelectionToClipboards(
4251	                        sel,
4252	                        &.{.standard},
4253	                        .mixed,
4254	                    );
4255	                }
4256	
4257	                try self.setSelection(null);
4258	                try self.queueRender();
4259	            },
4260	            .@"copy-or-paste" => if (self.io.terminal.screens.active.selection) |sel| {
4261	                try self.copySelectionToClipboards(
4262	                    sel,
4263	                    &.{.standard},
4264	                    .mixed,
4265	                );
4266	                try self.setSelection(null);
4267	                try self.queueRender();
4268	            } else {
4269	                // Pasting can trigger a lock grab in complete clipboard
4270	                // request so we need to unlock.
4271	                self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
4272	                defer self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
4273	                _ = try self.startClipboardRequest(.standard, .{ .paste = .standard });
4274	
4275	                // We don't need to clear selection because we didn't have
4276	                // one to begin with.
4277	            },
4278	            .paste => {
4279	                // Before we yield the lock, clear our selection if we have
4280	                // one.
4281	                try self.setSelection(null);
4282	                try self.queueRender();
4283	
4284	                // Pasting can trigger a lock grab in complete clipboard
4285	                // request so we need to unlock.
4286	                self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
4287	                defer self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
4288	                _ = try self.startClipboardRequest(.standard, .{ .paste = .standard });
4289	            },
4290	        }
4291	
4292	        // Consume the event such that the context menu is not displayed.
4293	        return true;
4294	    }
4295	
4296	    return false;
4297	}
4300	fn maybePromptClick(self: *Surface) !bool {
4301	    const t: *terminal.Terminal = self.renderer_state.terminal;
4302	    const screen: *terminal.Screen = t.screens.active;
4303	
4304	    // If our screen doesn't handle any prompt clicks, then we never
4305	    // do anything.
4306	    if (screen.semantic_prompt.click == .none) return false;
4307	
4308	    // If cursor-click-to-move is disabled, we don't do any prompt clicking.
4309	    if (!self.config.cursor_click_to_move) return false;
4310	
4311	    // If our cursor isn't currently at a prompt then we don't handle
4312	    // prompt clicks because we can't move if we're not in a prompt!
4313	    if (!t.cursorIsAtPrompt()) return false;
4314	
4315	    // A press reported under mouse tracking resets the gesture, and its
4316	    // release must be reported too rather than moving the cursor.
4317	    if (self.mouse.selection_gesture.left_click_count == 0) return false;
4318	
4319	    // If the left click moved away from its pressed cell then releasing the
4320	    // mouse completes the drag gesture and we don't do prompt moving.
4321	    if (self.mouse.selection_gesture.left_click_dragged) return false;
4322	
4323	    // If we have a selection currently, then releasing the mouse completes
4324	    // the selection and we don't do prompt moving.
4325	    if (screen.selection != null) return false;
4326	
4327	    // Get the pin for our mouse click.
4328	    const pos = try self.rt_surface.getCursorPos();
4329	    const pos_vp = self.posToViewport(pos.x, pos.y);
4330	    const click_pin: terminal.Pin = pin: {
4331	        const pin = screen.pages.pin(.{
4332	            .viewport = .{
4333	                .x = pos_vp.x,
4334	                .y = pos_vp.y,
4335	            },
4336	        }) orelse {
4337	            // See mouseButtonCallback for explanation
4338	            if (comptime std.debug.runtime_safety) unreachable;
4339	            return false;
4340	        };
4341	
4342	        break :pin pin;
4343	    };
4344	
4345	    // Get our cursor's most current prompt.
4346	    const prompt_pin: terminal.Pin = prompt_pin: {
4347	        var it = screen.cursor.page_pin.promptIterator(
4348	            .left_up,
4349	            null,
4350	        );
4351	        break :prompt_pin it.next() orelse {
4352	            // This shouldn't be possible because we asserted we're at
4353	            // a prompt above, so we MUST find some prompt in a left_up search.
4354	            log.warn("cursor is at prompt but no prompt found", .{});
4355	            if (comptime std.debug.runtime_safety) unreachable;
4356	            return false;
4357	        };
4358	    };
4359	
4360	    // If our mouse click is before the prompt, we don't move.
4361	    // We DO ALLOW clicks AFTER the prompt, specifically with Kitty's
4362	    // click_events=1 since we rely on the shell to validate out of
4363	    // bounds clicks. This matches Kitty's logic as best I can tell.
4364	    if (click_pin.before(prompt_pin)) return false;
4365	
4366	    // At this point we've established:
4367	    // - Screen supports prompt clicks
4368	    // - Cursor is at a prompt
4369	    // - Click is at or below our prompt
4370	    switch (screen.semantic_prompt.click) {
4371	        // Guarded at the start of this function
4372	        .none => unreachable,
4373	
4374	        .click_events => |v| {
4375	            // For the event, we always send a left-click press event.
4376	            // This matches what Kitty sends.
4377	            const key: u8, const y: u32 = switch (v) {
4378	                .absolute => .{ 1, pos_vp.y +| 1 },
4379	                .relative => .{
4380	                    2,
4381	                    promptClickRelativeRow(&screen.pages, prompt_pin, click_pin),
4382	                },
4383	            };
4384	            var data: termio.Message.WriteReq.Small.Array = undefined;
4385	            const resp = try std.mem.print(
4386	                &data,
4387	                "\x1B[<0;{d};{d}M",
4388	                .{ pos_vp.x + 1, y },
4389	            );
4390	
4391	            // Not that noisy since this only happens on prompt clicks.
4392	            log.debug(
4393	                "sending click_events={} event=ESC{s}",
4394	                .{ key, resp[1..] },
4395	            );
4396	
4397	            // Ask our IO thread to write the data
4398	            self.queueIo(.{ .write_small = .{
4399	                .data = data,
4400	                .len = @intCast(resp.len),
4401	            } }, .locked);
4402	        },
4403	
4404	        .cl => {
4405	            const left_arrow = if (t.modes.get(.cursor_keys)) "\x1bOD" else "\x1b[D";
4406	            const right_arrow = if (t.modes.get(.cursor_keys)) "\x1bOC" else "\x1b[C";
4407	
4408	            const move = screen.promptClickMove(click_pin);
4409	            for (0..move.left) |_| {
4410	                self.queueIo(
4411	                    .{ .write_stable = left_arrow },
4412	                    .locked,
4413	                );
4414	            }
4415	            for (0..move.right) |_| {
4416	                self.queueIo(
4417	                    .{ .write_stable = right_arrow },
4418	                    .locked,
4419	                );
4420	            }
4421	        },
4422	    }
4423	
4424	    return true;
4425	}
4553	fn processLinks(self: *Surface, pos: apprt.CursorPos) !bool {
4554	    const link = try self.linkAtPos(pos) orelse return false;
4555	    switch (link.action) {
4556	        .open => {
4557	            const str = try self.io.terminal.screens.active.selectionString(self.alloc, .{
4558	                .sel = link.selection,
4559	                .trim = false,
4560	            });
4561	            defer self.alloc.free(str);
4562	
4563	            const resolved_path = try self.resolvePathForOpening(str);
4564	            defer if (resolved_path) |p| self.alloc.free(p);
4565	
4566	            const url_to_open = resolved_path orelse str;
4567	            try self.openUrl(.{ .kind = .unknown, .url = url_to_open });
4568	        },
4569	
4570	        ._open_osc8 => {
4571	            const uri = self.osc8URI(link.selection.start()) orelse {
4572	                log.warn("failed to get URI for OSC8 hyperlink", .{});
4573	                return false;
4574	            };
4575	            try self.openUrl(.{ .kind = .osc8, .url = uri });
4576	        },
4577	    }
4578	
4579	    return true;
4580	}
4941	pub fn performBindingAction(self: *Surface, action: input.Binding.Action) !bool {
4942	    // Forward app-scoped actions to the app. Some app-scoped actions are
4943	    // special-cased here because they do some special things when performed
4944	    // from the surface.
4945	    if (action.scoped(.app)) |app_action| {
4946	        switch (app_action) {
4947	            .new_window => try self.app.newWindow(
4948	                self.rt_app,
4949	                .{ .parent = self },
4950	            ),
4951	
4952	            // Undo and redo both support both surface and app targeting.
4953	            // If we are triggering on a surface then we perform the
4954	            // action with the surface target.
4955	            .undo => return try self.rt_app.performAction(
4956	                .{ .surface = self },
4957	                .undo,
4958	                {},
4959	            ),
4960	
4961	            .redo => return try self.rt_app.performAction(
4962	                .{ .surface = self },
4963	                .redo,
4964	                {},
4965	            ),
4966	
4967	            else => try self.app.performAction(
4968	                self.rt_app,
4969	                action.scoped(.app).?,
4970	            ),
4971	        }
4972	        return true;
4973	    }
4974	
4975	    switch (action.scoped(.surface).?) {
4976	        .csi, .esc => |data| {
4977	            // We need to send the CSI/ESC sequence as a single write request.
4978	            // If you split it across two then the shell can interpret it
4979	            // as two literals.
4980	            var buf: [128]u8 = undefined;
4981	            const full_data = switch (action) {
4982	                .csi => try std.mem.print(&buf, "\x1b[{s}", .{data}),
4983	                .esc => try std.mem.print(&buf, "\x1b{s}", .{data}),
4984	                else => unreachable,
4985	            };
4986	            self.queueIo(try termio.Message.writeReq(
4987	                self.alloc,
4988	                full_data,
4989	            ), .unlocked);
4990	
4991	            // CSI/ESC triggers a scroll.
4992	            {
4993	                self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
4994	                defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
4995	                self.scrollToBottom() catch |err| {
4996	                    log.warn("error scrolling to bottom err={}", .{err});
4997	                };
4998	            }
4999	        },
5000	
5001	        .text => |data| {
5002	            var stack_buf: [256]u8 = undefined;
5003	            var stack: std.heap.BufferFirstAllocator = .init(&stack_buf, self.alloc);
5004	            const alloc = stack.allocator();
5005	            const buf = try alloc.alloc(u8, data.len);
5006	            defer alloc.free(buf);
5007	            const text = configpkg.string.parse(buf, data) catch |err| {
5008	                log.warn(
5009	                    "error parsing text binding text={s} err={}",
5010	                    .{ data, err },
5011	                );
5012	                return true;
5013	            };
5014	            self.queueIo(try termio.Message.writeReq(
5015	                self.alloc,
5016	                text,
5017	            ), .unlocked);
5018	
5019	            // Text triggers a scroll.
5020	            {
5021	                self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
5022	                defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
5023	                self.scrollToBottom() catch |err| {
5024	                    log.warn("error scrolling to bottom err={}", .{err});
5025	                };
5026	            }
5027	        },
5028	
5029	        .cursor_key => |ck| {
5030	            // We send a different sequence depending on if we're
5031	            // in cursor keys mode. We're in "normal" mode if cursor
5032	            // keys mode is NOT set.
5033	            const normal = normal: {
5034	                self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
5035	                defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
5036	
5037	                // With the lock held, we must scroll to the bottom.
5038	                // We always scroll to the bottom for these inputs.
5039	                self.scrollToBottom() catch |err| {
5040	                    log.warn("error scrolling to bottom err={}", .{err});
5041	                };
5042	
5043	                break :normal !self.io.terminal.modes.get(.cursor_keys);
5044	            };
5045	
5046	            if (normal) {
5047	                self.queueIo(.{ .write_stable = ck.normal }, .unlocked);
5048	            } else {
5049	                self.queueIo(.{ .write_stable = ck.application }, .unlocked);
5050	            }
5051	        },
5052	
5053	        .reset => {
5054	            self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
5055	            defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
5056	            self.renderer_state.terminal.fullReset();
5057	        },
5059	        .start_search => {
5060	            // To save resources, we don't actually start a search here,
5061	            // we just notify the apprt. The real thread will start when
5062	            // the first needles are set.
5063	            return try self.rt_app.performAction(
5064	                .{ .surface = self },
5065	                .start_search,
5066	                .{ .needle = "" },
5067	            );
5068	        },
5069	
5070	        .search_selection => {
5071	            const selection = try self.selectionString(self.alloc) orelse return false;
5072	            defer self.alloc.free(selection);
5073	            return try self.rt_app.performAction(
5074	                .{ .surface = self },
5075	                .start_search,
5076	                .{ .needle = selection },
5077	            );
5078	        },
5079	
5080	        .end_search => {
5081	            // We only return that this was performed if we actually
5082	            // stopped a search, but we also send the apprt end_search so
5083	            // that GUIs can clean up stale stuff.
5084	            const performed = self.search != null;
5085	
5086	            if (self.search) |*s| {
5087	                s.deinit();
5088	                self.search = null;
5089	            }
5090	
5091	            _ = try self.rt_app.performAction(
5092	                .{ .surface = self },
5093	                .end_search,
5094	                {},
5095	            );
5096	
5097	            return performed;
5098	        },
5099	
5100	        .search => |text| search: {
5101	            const s: *Search = if (self.search) |*s| s else init: {
5102	                // If we're stopping the search and we had no prior search,
5103	                // then there is nothing to do.
5104	                if (text.len == 0) return false;
5105	
5106	                // We need to assign directly to self.search because we need
5107	                // a stable pointer back to the thread state.
5108	                self.search = .{
5109	                    .state = try .init(self.alloc, .{
5110	                        .mutex = self.renderer_state.mutex,
5111	                        .terminal = self.renderer_state.terminal,
5112	                        .event_cb = &searchCallback,
5113	                        .event_userdata = self,
5114	                    }),
5115	                    .thread = undefined,
5116	                };
5117	                const s: *Search = &self.search.?;
5118	                errdefer s.state.deinit();
5119	
5120	                s.thread = try .spawn(
5121	                    .{},
5122	                    terminal.search.Thread.threadMain,
5123	                    .{&s.state},
5124	                );
5125	                s.thread.setName(global.io(), "search") catch {};
5126	
5127	                break :init s;
5128	            };
5129	
5130	            // Zero-length text means stop searching.
5131	            if (text.len == 0) {
5132	                s.deinit();
5133	                self.search = null;
5134	                break :search;
5135	            }
5136	
5137	            _ = s.state.mailbox.push(
5138	                global.io(),
5139	                .{ .change_needle = try .init(
5140	                    self.alloc,
5141	                    text,
5142	                ) },
5143	                .forever,
5144	            );
5145	            s.state.wakeup.notify() catch {};
5146	        },
5147	
5148	        .navigate_search => |nav| {
5149	            const s: *Search = if (self.search) |*s| s else return false;
5150	            _ = s.state.mailbox.push(
5151	                global.io(),
5152	                .{ .select = switch (nav) {
5153	                    .next => .next,
5154	                    .previous => .prev,
5155	                } },
5156	                .forever,
5157	            );
5158	            s.state.wakeup.notify() catch {};
5159	        },
5161	        .copy_to_clipboard => |format| {
5162	            self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
5163	            defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
5164	
5165	            if (self.io.terminal.screens.active.selection) |sel| {
5166	                try self.copySelectionToClipboards(
5167	                    sel,
5168	                    &.{.standard},
5169	                    format,
5170	                );
5171	
5172	                // Clear the selection if configured to do so.
5173	                if (self.config.selection_clear_on_copy) {
5174	                    if (self.setSelection(null)) {
5175	                        self.queueRender() catch |err| {
5176	                            log.warn("failed to queue render after clear selection err={}", .{err});
5177	                        };
5178	                    } else |err| {
5179	                        log.warn("failed to clear selection after copy err={}", .{err});
5180	                    }
5181	                }
5182	
5183	                return true;
5184	            }
5185	
5186	            return false;
5187	        },
5240	        .paste_from_clipboard => return (try self.startClipboardRequest(
5241	            .standard,
5242	            .{ .paste = .standard },
5243	        )) == .started,
5244	
5245	        .paste_from_selection => return (try self.startClipboardRequest(
5246	            .selection,
5247	            .{ .paste = .selection },
5248	        )) == .started,
5494	        .new_split => |direction| return try self.rt_app.performAction(
5495	            .{ .surface = self },
5496	            .new_split,
5497	            switch (direction) {
5498	                .right => .right,
5499	                .left => .left,
5500	                .down => .down,
5501	                .up => .up,
5502	                .auto => if (self.size.screen.width > self.size.screen.height)
5503	                    .right
5504	                else
5505	                    .down,
5506	            },
5507	        ),
5555	        .toggle_readonly => {
5556	            self.readonly = !self.readonly;
5557	            _ = try self.rt_app.performAction(
5558	                .{ .surface = self },
5559	                .readonly,
5560	                if (self.readonly) .on else .off,
5561	            );
5562	            return true;
5563	        },
5635	        .select_all => {
5636	            self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
5637	            defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
5638	
5639	            const sel = self.io.terminal.screens.active.selectAll();
5640	            if (sel) |s| {
5641	                try self.setSelectionAndCopy(s);
5642	                try self.queueRender();
5643	            }
5644	        },
5645	
5646	        .inspector => |mode| return try self.rt_app.performAction(
5647	            .{ .surface = self },
5648	            .inspector,
5649	            switch (mode) {
5650	                inline else => |tag| @field(
5651	                    apprt.action.Inspector,
5652	                    @tagName(tag),
5653	                ),
5654	            },
5655	        ),
5656	
5657	        .close_surface => self.close(),
5658	
5659	        .close_window => return try self.rt_app.performAction(
5660	            .{ .surface = self },
5661	            .close_window,
5662	            {},
5663	        ),
5824	fn closingAction(action: input.Binding.Action) bool {
5825	    return switch (action) {
5826	        .close_surface,
5827	        .close_window,
5828	        .close_tab,
5829	        => true,
5830	
5831	        else => false,
5832	    };
5833	}
6017	pub fn completeClipboardRequest(
6018	    self: *Surface,
6019	    req: apprt.ClipboardRequest,
6020	    complete: CompleteClipboard,
6021	) !void {
6022	    switch (req) {
6023	        .paste => try self.completeClipboardPaste(
6024	            clipboardTextContent(complete.contents) orelse "",
6025	            complete.confirmed,
6026	        ),
6027	
6028	        .list => |clipboard| if (!try self.completeClipboardPasteEvent(
6029	            clipboard,
6030	            complete.available,
6031	        )) {
6032	            log.debug("mode 5522 paste event was not written", .{});
6033	        },
6219	fn startClipboardRequest(
6220	    self: *Surface,
6221	    loc: apprt.Clipboard,
6222	    req: apprt.ClipboardRequest,
6223	) !apprt.ClipboardReadResult {
6224	    const effective_req: apprt.ClipboardRequest = switch (req) {
6225	        .paste => |clipboard| effective: {
6226	            // Snapshot the mode before asking the apprt for clipboard data.
6227	            // Event pastes request only a MIME listing, while ordinary
6228	            // pastes request the text representation as before.
6229	            self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
6230	            const event = self.io.terminal.modes.get(.kitty_paste_events);
6231	            self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
6232	
6233	            break :effective if (event)
6234	                .{ .list = clipboard }
6235	            else
6236	                req;
6237	        },
6238	        else => req,
6239	    };
6260	
6261	    return try self.rt_surface.clipboardRequest(loc, effective_req);
6262	}
6263	
6264	fn completeClipboardPaste(
6265	    self: *Surface,
6266	    data: []const u8,
6267	    allow_unsafe: bool,
6268	) !void {
6269	    if (data.len == 0) return;
6270	
6271	    const encode_opts: input.paste.Options = encode_opts: {
6272	        self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
6273	        defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
6274	        const opts: input.paste.Options = .fromTerminal(&self.io.terminal);
6275	
6276	        // If we have paste protection enabled, we detect unsafe pastes and return
6277	        // an error. The error approach allows apprt to attempt to complete the paste
6278	        // before falling back to requesting confirmation.
6279	        //
6280	        // We do not do this for bracketed pastes because bracketed pastes are
6281	        // by definition safe since they're framed.
6282	        const unsafe = unsafe: {
6283	            // If we've disabled paste protection then we always allow the paste.
6284	            if (!self.config.clipboard_paste_protection) break :unsafe false;
6285	
6286	            // If we're allowed to paste unsafe data then we always allow the paste.
6287	            // This is set during confirmation usually.
6288	            if (allow_unsafe) break :unsafe false;
6289	
6290	            if (opts.bracketed) {
6291	                // If we're bracketed and the paste contains and ending
6292	                // bracket then something naughty might be going on and we
6293	                // never trust it.
6294	                if (std.mem.indexOf(u8, data, "\x1B[201~") != null) break :unsafe true;
6295	
6296	                // If we are bracketed and configured to trust that then the
6297	                // paste is not unsafe.
6298	                if (self.config.clipboard_paste_bracketed_safe) break :unsafe false;
6299	            }
6300	
6301	            break :unsafe !input.paste.isSafe(data);
6302	        };
6303	
6304	        if (unsafe) {
6305	            log.info("potentially unsafe paste detected, rejecting until confirmation", .{});
6306	            return error.UnsafePaste;
6307	        }
6308	
6309	        // With the lock held, we must scroll to the bottom.
6310	        // We always scroll to the bottom for these inputs.
6311	        self.scrollToBottom() catch |err| {
6312	            log.warn("error scrolling to bottom err={}", .{err});
6313	        };
6314	
6315	        break :encode_opts opts;
6316	    };
6317	
6318	    // Encode the data. In most cases this doesn't require any
6319	    // copies, so we optimize for that case.
6320	    var data_duped: ?[]u8 = null;
6321	    const vecs = input.paste.encode(data, encode_opts) catch |err| switch (err) {
6322	        error.MutableRequired => vecs: {
6323	            const buf: []u8 = try self.alloc.dupe(u8, data);
6324	            errdefer self.alloc.free(buf);
6325	            data_duped = buf;
6326	            break :vecs input.paste.encode(buf, encode_opts);
6327	        },
6328	    };
6329	    defer if (data_duped) |v| {
6330	        // This code path means the data did require a copy and mutation.
6331	        // We must free it.
6332	        self.alloc.free(v);
6333	    };
6334	
6335	    for (vecs) |vec| if (vec.len > 0) {
6336	        self.queueIo(try termio.Message.writeReq(
6337	            self.alloc,
6338	            vec,
6339	        ), .unlocked);
6340	    };
6341	}
6342	
6343	/// Send a Kitty clipboard-protocol paste event when mode 5522 is enabled.
6344	/// The event only lists the available MIME types; it does not read any of
6345	/// their data. The shared terminal paste implementation generates and records
6346	/// the one-time password used by the program's follow-up OSC 5522 read.
6347	fn completeClipboardPasteEvent(
6348	    self: *Surface,
6349	    clipboard: apprt.Clipboard,
6350	    available: []const []const u8,
6351	) !bool {
6352	    if (self.readonly) return false;
6353	
6354	    const kitty_clipboard = terminal.kitty.clipboard;
6355	    const location: terminal.clipboard.Location = switch (clipboard) {
6356	        .standard => .standard,
6357	        .selection => .selection,
6358	        .primary => .primary,
6359	    };
6360	
6361	    // The protocol implementation caps listings at this size too. Cap here
6362	    // so the temporary Content array stays on the stack.
6363	    var contents_buf: [kitty_clipboard.max_listing_mimes]terminal.clipboard.Content = undefined;
6364	    const contents_len = @min(available.len, contents_buf.len);
6365	    for (available[0..contents_len], contents_buf[0..contents_len]) |mime, *content| {
6366	        content.* = .{ .mime = mime, .data = "" };
6367	    }
6368	
6369	    var aw: std.Io.Writer.Allocating = .init(self.alloc);
6370	    defer aw.deinit();
6371	
6372	    self.renderer_state.mutex.lockUncancelable(global.io());
6373	    defer self.renderer_state.mutex.unlock(global.io());
6374	
6375	    const pasted = try terminal.paste.paste(.{
6376	        .terminal = &self.io.terminal,
6377	        .alloc = self.alloc,
6378	        .writer = &aw.writer,
6379	        .kitty_clipboard = .{
6380	            .grants = &self.io.terminal_stream.handler.kitty_clipboard_grants,
6381	            .io = global.io(),
6382	        },
6383	    }, .{
6384	        .source = .{ .clipboard = location },
6385	        .contents = .{ .memory = contents_buf[0..contents_len] },
6386	        // A paste event discloses no clipboard data, so unsafe-text
6387	        // confirmation does not apply. If mode 5522 is reset, the empty
6388	        // stand-in representations cause the shared helper to write nothing.
6389	        .allow_unsafe = true,
6390	    });
6391	    if (!pasted) return false;
6392	
6393	    self.queueIo(.{ .write_alloc = .{
6394	        .alloc = self.alloc,
6395	        .data = try aw.toOwnedSlice(),
6396	    } }, .locked);
6397	    return true;
6398	}
